# Run 001 (2026-11-10): adversarial review of the charger IC search decision document

## 1. Method

The project review loop specifies a Codex round-1 reviewer. Codex could not run in this round. The installed Codex CLI 0.146.0 rejects the configured model `gpt-6-astra` ("requires a newer version of Codex"), and the ChatGPT account rejects every older model tried (gpt-5.5, gpt-5.4, gpt-5.3-codex, gpt-5-codex: `model_not_found`). Codex thread 01a1c6f0-260b-7530-a1b3-e99a0c7b8a4f failed at turn start. Four independent Claude adversarial reviewers therefore stood in for Codex round 1, each with one lens: decision logic and scoring (Fable), datasheet facts and numbers (Opus), system safety and buck versus buck-boost (Opus), and coverage, consistency and writing (Opus).

The remaining structure of the loop was kept. A merge agent combined 42 raw findings into 36. One `finding-verifier` agent per finding checked each against the primary datasheets and the working documents, with the reviewer severity withheld (quantitative findings to Opus, adjudication to Fable, wording to Sonnet). Two second-pass reviewers, given the 36 findings, raised 11 new ones, which were verified in the same way. A counter-review agent then answered every verdict with CONCEDE, DEFEND or REFINE. The round has 47 findings and used 55 agents.

## 2. Outcome

Final severities: 0 critical, 5 major, 40 minor, 2 none. Status: 45 accepted, 2 rejected, 0 settled-caveat. No verifier marked any finding as changing the recommendation (retain the BQ25798) or the shortlist order.

## 3. Adjudication of the major findings

### CIS-R1-04

**Finding.** "The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound after a register loss exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop"; BQ25798 "1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal)" vs BQ25773 "1.39 A ... exceeds by 0.19 A (C)", "a hazard that the BQ25798 does not have". Table 5.1: BQ25798 "3.10 A with the reference-biased ceiling ... Met at the floor" vs BQ25690 "2.62 A at the ceiling minimum" and BQ25773 "3.04 A (ICHG low corner)" (not met).

**Problem.** The two sides of each comparison are not computed to the same tolerance basis, although section 1.1 defines the delivered figure with every tolerance at its low corner. (a) Delivered charge: 3.10 A is the BQ25798 NOMINAL ICHG (dossier_BQ25798.md 11.3, 'ICHG-limited'); the input path is not limiting nominally (1.55 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4 = 3.18 A). SLUSDV2C p.11 (ICHG_ACC, buck, VBAT 8 V, TJ -40 to 85 C) gives -6.5/+3.5 % at 2 A and nothing above 2 A ([D798] 11.1); at that corner the BQ25798 delivers 3.1 x 0.935 = 2.90 A, 0.20 A short, a larger shortfall than the BQ25773's -0.06 A. The target applies the +3.5 % upper tolerance for headroom (line 248: 3.21 A), so the omission of the lower side is one-sided. The ILIM_HIZ pin-to-current conversion has no published accuracy ([D798] 7.1/7.2 item 3, B: S1-B6); an IINDPM-class -10 % on the 1.55 A floor gives about 1.40 A and 2.8-2.86 A delivered. The reference-biased 1.55-1.61 A band appears in no calc script (calc/bq798_ccb.py computes only the REGN divider). (b) FC bound: [D798] 7.2 item 1 grades 1.15 A 'C nominal: ideal Eq.1, no pin-to-current accuracy. The 0.047 A margin to the arm drop is smaller than any plausible conversion error and stays unverified until S1-B6'; a pin-conversion error worse than +4.7 % (0.047/1.003) puts the BQ25798 above 1.20 A. The BQ25773 1.39 A includes its published +-200 mA pin accuracy (SLUSEK7 EC p.12) and the BQ25690 1.285-1.715 A band includes the published KILIM spread. The 0.71-1.00 A FC band was computed for the REGN-referenced divider while the scored build ([SCO] Table 2) uses the reference-biased ceiling, and [D798] item 1 leaves open whether the FC leg is also reference-biased, so the FC band of the scored build is uncomputed. The BQ25773 POR bound is 0.15 A (CHARGE_CURRENT 0 at POR); its 1.39 A needs a register loss, which corresponds to a BQ25798 stray IINDPM write, not a POR. Consequently the "only part" claim in 6.1, headline reasons 2 and 4 and succession reason 1 compare a tolerance-free nominal baseline against worst-case corners for the challengers, and the "None" entry in the HR1 documented-contrary-fact row hides a B.

**Verification (PARTLY_CONFIRMED, major).** Delivered charge, BQ25798, reference-biased floor 1.55 A, VINDPM floor 18.62 V, efficiency 0.925 (dossier Fig 6-3 reading, 750 kHz):
- Input-limited current: 1.55 x 18.62 = 28.861 W; x 0.925 = 26.70 W; / 8.4 V = 3.178 A. The input path does not limit at nominal.
- ICHG low corner, 2 A-row -6.5 % applied as a percentage: 3.10 x 0.935 = 2.899 A. This is 0.20 A short of 3.1 A, more than the BQ25773 shortfall of 3.04 - 3.10 = -0.06 A.
- ICHG low corner, the 2 A-row error held at -130 mA absolute: 3.10 - 0.13 = 2.97 A, which is still short.
- Any negative ICHG error at a 3.10 A setting gives less than 3.10 A, so "Met at the floor" fails under the definition at :125-127 for any non-zero lower tolerance.
- Pin-conversion proxy of -10 % (B): 1.395 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4 = 2.860 A.

Setting that closes the ICHG side: S x 0.935 >= 3.10 gives S >= 3.3155 A, so 3.32 A in 10 mA steps, and 3.32 x 0.935 = 3.104 A. min(3.104, 3.178) = 3.104 A >= 3.10 A, at nominal pin conversion only.
- High corner: 3.32 x 1.035 = 3.436 A.
- Headroom term at 100 mOhm: 0.344 V instead of 0.321 V.
- Loss at 3.1 A is unchanged in class, because the input path limits at 3.18 A.

FC bound:
- 1.003 + 0.15 = 1.153 A, margin 0.047 A to 1.20 A.
- The error that consumes the margin is +4.7 % (0.047 / 1.003).
- The IINDPM proxy upper error at 1 A is 0 % (1000 mA maximum), so the margin holds under the proxy.
- The BQ25773 pin-accuracy class, +-200 mA at about 1 A (about +-20 %), would give 1.20 + 0.15 = 1.35 A on the BQ25798. This is unknown, not shown.

BQ25773 at POR: CHARGE_CURRENT 0, so the bus draw is 0.15 A aux only. The 1.39 A needs a partial register loss.

**Severity rationale.** The exposure class is silently wrong published numbers. Under the document's own stated basis (:125-127), the BQ25798 delivers no more than 2.97 A (absolute-error extrapolation) or 2.90 A (percentage extrapolation). It is published as "3.10 A ... Met at the floor" in three places, and that claim supports headline reason 4, which says only the challengers miss 3.1 A. The computed bound is a 0.20 A shortfall against a 0.06 A shortfall for the BQ25773, so the ranking inside reason 4 inverts between those two parts.

The finding does not reach critical, and it does not change the recommendation:
- No criterion scores delivered current (target :553-555), so the totals and the order do not move.
- The BQ25798 closes its gap with a register setting, as the BQ25773 does.
- The BQ25690's structural shortfall remains.

Part (b) is a basis inconsistency only. The target discloses "C nominal", and the published proxy gives no positive error, so no exposure path to the arm drop is shown. Alone, part (b) would cap at minor.

**Counter-review (REFINE, major).** Major is upheld on part (a) and I accept the verifier's narrowing of part (b). (a): under the document's own basis (:125-127, every tolerance at its low corner) the BQ25798 delivers min(3.10 x 0.935 = 2.90 A, 1.55 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4 = 3.18 A) = 2.90 A, or 2.97 A on the absolute -130 mA reading; any non-zero lower tolerance makes 'Met at the floor' false, and the 0.20 A shortfall exceeds the BQ25773's published 0.06 A, inverting the comparison inside reason 4. The 3.32 A setting that closes it (3.32 x 0.935 = 3.104 A) is the symmetric remedy the document grants the BQ25773 and withholds from itself. (b): the IINDPM proxy at 1 A has no high-side error, so no exposure path to the arm drop is shown; the BQ25798 1.15 A is 'C nominal' as disclosed and the 'None' contrary-fact cell should read 'None documented (above-2 A ICHG accuracy U)'. Refinement: this finding must be edited jointly with S01 and R1-07. S01 shows 3.1 A is the BQ25798 image setting, not the requirement, so the corrected Table 5.1 column should be 'against the 3.10 A image setting' with all three low-corner figures (2.90 A U extrapolated, 2.77 A, 2.62 A) and HR1 as the pass/fail column; otherwise fixing the BQ25798 cell alone re-creates a one-sided table.

**Agreed fix.** 1. Table 5.1 (:534), BQ25798 row, "Delivered at the threshold floor" cell. Replace with: "3.10 A at the nominal ICHG (input-ceiling limit 3.18 A with the reference-biased ceiling); 2.90 A at the ICHG low corner if the 2 A-row -6.5 % applies at 3.1 A (U: no ICHG accuracy is published above 2 A); 2.86 A with a -10 % pin error (B, S1-B6); an ICHG setting of 3.32 A gives 3.10 A at the low corner". In the "Against 3.1 A" cell, replace "Met at the floor" with "Met at nominal; not met at the extrapolated low corner (-0.20 A) unless ICHG is set to 3.32 A".

2. Section 0.1 reason 4 (:76-80). Replace "Two challengers do not reach 3.1 A ... the BQ25798 delivers 3.10 A with the reference-biased ceiling" with: "At the low tolerance corner the BQ25690 delivers 2.62 A (input-limited at its ceiling minimum). The BQ25773 delivers 3.04 A at its 3.200 A setting and the BQ25798 delivers about 2.90 A at its 3.10 A setting (2 A-row ICHG tolerance extrapolated, U). Setting changes close both gaps (BQ25773 3.264 A, BQ25798 3.32 A). The BQ25690 gap cannot close without a regen ceiling above the HR4 hard-stop row."

3. Make the same change in section 5.1 (:571-572), Table 6.0b row :848 and the summary at :553-555. The summary should name the BQ25690 as the only structural shortfall.

4. Section 2.2 (:248). If the 3.32 A setting is adopted, change "3.21 A (3.10 A plus the +3.5 % ICHG tolerance)" to "3.44 A (3.32 A plus +3.5 %)" and recompute section 2.3 with 0.344 V.

5. FC bound, at :578, :853, :69-72 and :857-859:
   - Replace "1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal)" with "1.15 A nominal, margin 0.05 A; pin-to-current accuracy unpublished (U, S1-B6); the IINDPM_ACC proxy at 1 A (880-1000 mA) adds no high-side error (B)".
   - Replace ":857-859 The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound ... exceeds" with "The BQ25773 is the only part with a published FC bound above the 1.20 A arm drop; the BQ25798 bound is nominal until S1-B6".
   - Reason 2 (:69-72): change "a hazard that the BQ25798 does not have" to "a documented hazard; the corresponding BQ25798 figure is nominal (S1-B6)".
   - Set the HR1 "documented contrary fact" cell for the BQ25798 to "None documented (above-2 A ICHG accuracy U)".

6. Calc record: add the reference-biased band (2.5 V +-0.5 %, 0.1 % ratio, 5 kOhm Thevenin, +-1.5 uA leakage) and the 3.32 A setting check to calc/bq798_ccb.py. The check is min(S x 0.935, 1.55 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4) >= 3.10.

Proof: 3.32 x 0.935 = 3.104 A >= 3.10 A, and 3.178 A >= 3.104 A, so the input path is not binding. The fix is a document correction. The follow-on skill is test in the calc-script sense (pin the band in bq798_ccb.py). No firmware, protocol or coeffs regeneration is triggered.

### CIS-R1-06

**Finding.** BQ25798 FC bus draw after a charger POR or a register loss is 1.15 A (margin 0.05 A to the 1.20 A arm drop). The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound after a register loss exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop and the 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX, a hazard the BQ25798 does not have.

**Problem.** The 1.15 A figure assumes ILIM_HIZ is re-sampled continuously. That is P6, which is open (B, S1-B6). [EVAL] line 655 states the bad branch: 'If only at qualification, a Run-entry qualification in regen mode keeps the regen clamp through an FC window (the FC bound is then IINDPM, written and read back, plus firmware)'. The branch is reachable under the always-powered design, because the charger qualifies at Run entry with REGEN closed on the regen leg. In that branch the BQ25798's hardware FC bound is the regen ceiling, 1.55-1.61 A ([D798] 7.2): above the arm drop, above P_MAX and above OC_FC 1.4 A, and larger than the 1.39 A held against the BQ25773. After a register loss (IINDPM), the BQ25798 is then in the same hazard class as the BQ25773, and worse. Table 6.0b grades the BQ25798 row 'C nominal' without the P6 condition, and reason 2 counts the hazard against the BQ25773 alone.

**Verification (CONFIRMED, major).** Good branch (ILIM_HIZ re-sampled after Q3 switches, FC divider in force): 1.003 A (FC band maximum, dossier 7.2) + 0.15 A aux = 1.153 A; margin to the 1.20 A arm drop 0.047 A. This reproduces the target's 1.15 A / 0.05 A.

Bad branch (sampled once, at the regen Run-entry qualification; IINDPM register lost so the effective limit falls back to the held clamp): reference-biased clamp 1.55-1.61 A + 0.15 A aux = 1.70-1.76 A bus draw in an FC window.
- vs 1.20 A arm drop: exceeds by 0.50-0.56 A (the BQ25773 exceeds by 0.19 A).
- vs 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX: exceeds by 0.45-0.51 A.
- vs 1.4 A OC_FC raw-sample latch: exceeds by 0.30-0.36 A, so ERR_OC_FC fires (fault path intact).
- vs the 1.39 A held against the BQ25773: larger by 0.31-0.37 A.
With the REGN-divider option instead (1.42-1.81 A): up to 1.81 + 0.15 = 1.96 A.

The finding's own figure "1.55-1.61 A" omits the 0.15 A aux that both the target's 1.15 A and the BQ25773's 1.39 A include; the like-for-like bound is 1.70-1.76 A.

Register-loss paths for the BQ25798 IINDPM: REG_RST, a stray write to REG06, or EN_EXTILIM = 0 (REG14[1]); the watchdog does NOT reset REG06 (Table 7-18 p.63), unlike ICHG (Table 7-16 p.61). After a REG_RST the POR value 3000 mA lands at the held clamp per section 7.3.4.3.

Scoring impact: zero. score.py carries no HR4/FC-bound term; S3 already scores the BQ25798 at 2 (B) citing P6 (dossier_BQ25798.md:586, [CRIT] S3 band 3 requires continuous re-sampling). The 40.3 / 49.0 totals and the 8.7-point margin are unchanged.

**Severity rationale.** Computed bound: 1.70-1.76 A hardware FC-window bound after an IINDPM loss in the sampled-once branch, against the 1.15 A published, exceeding the 1.20 A arm drop by 0.56 A and the OC_FC latch by 0.36 A, and 0.31-0.37 A above the 1.39 A the document holds against the BQ25773. Reachability: the designed Run-entry sequence qualifies in the regen divider position and the Regen -> FC transition never re-qualifies, so the branch is the normal operating path whenever the datasheet-described sampled-once behaviour holds; the datasheet describes only that behaviour (section 7.3.4.3 "sets a high clamp ... after POR"), so the branch is at least as likely as the one the target assumes. Exposure class: a silently wrong published comparative number in one of the five stated reasons for the recommendation (reason 2) and in the HR table a reader would use to rank the parts; the hazard is counted against the BQ25773 alone while the BQ25798 shares it at a larger magnitude. Not critical: the OC_FC raw-sample latch (1.4 A) fires before any converter-kill or back-feed path, and the H-20 excursion above P_MAX is bounded to the latch time. Does not flip the recommendation: the score carries no HR4 term, S3 already discounts P6, and the BQ25773's tTRANS_CHG hazard stands on its own; the operator would, however, read reason 2 differently.

**Counter-review (REFINE, major).** Major confirmed; I accept the correction to my own figure. The like-for-like bound includes the 0.15 A aux the target's 1.15 A and the BQ25773's 1.39 A both carry: 1.55-1.61 A + 0.15 A = 1.70-1.76 A, above the 1.20 A arm drop by 0.50-0.56 A, above the 1.4 A OC_FC latch by 0.30-0.36 A, and 0.31-0.37 A above the figure held against the BQ25773 alone. Reachability holds: the designed Run-entry qualification is in the regen divider position and the Regen -> FC transition never re-qualifies, and SLUSDV2C 7.3.4.3 describes only the held-clamp behaviour, so the sampled-once branch is at least as likely as the one the target assumes. Not critical because the OC_FC raw-sample latch fires before any converter path and S3 already discounts P6 so no score moves. The four edits plus the scoring.md mirror are accepted; the register-loss path note (REG_RST, stray REG06 write, EN_EXTILIM = 0; watchdog does not reset REG06) belongs in the Table 6.0b cell.

**Agreed fix.** Four text edits in docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md, plus the mirror sentence in the working document.

1. Table 6.0b (line 853), BQ25798 cell of the row "HR4 FC bus draw after a charger POR or a register loss (limit: 1.20 A arm drop)": replace "1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal)" with "After a POR: 1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal). After an IINDPM loss: 1.15 A only if ILIM_HIZ is re-sampled after qualification (P6, S1-B6 open); if sampled once, the regen clamp latched at the Run-entry qualification persists through FC windows and the bound is 1.70-1.76 A (reference-biased 1.55-1.61 A + 0.15 A aux), above the arm drop by 0.56 A and the 1.4 A OC_FC latch by 0.36 A ([EVAL] 4.1; [D798] 7.2)". Row "HR4 status" (line 854): "C nominal" -> "C nominal (regen); conditional on S1-B6 (FC, register loss)".

2. Line 859: replace "The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound after a register loss exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop and the 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX." with "After a register loss the BQ25773 FC bound (1.39 A) exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop and the 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX. The BQ25798 FC bound after an IINDPM loss stays at 1.15 A only if ILIM_HIZ is re-sampled after qualification (P6, S1-B6); in the branch the datasheet describes (an ADC read at POR and at each qualification, held as an IINDPM clamp, SLUSDV2C section 7.3.4 step 6 and section 7.3.4.3 p.27) the bound is 1.70-1.76 A."

3. Section 0.1 reason 2 (lines 69-71): delete the clause "and its fuel-cell (FC) bus draw after a loss of its input-current register reaches 1.39 A, above the 1.20 A arm drop ([D773] sections 8.3, 11.4)" and cite only [D773] section 8.3; or, if it is kept, append "(the BQ25798 shares this hazard class at 1.70-1.76 A unless bench gate S1-B6 shows continuous ILIM_HIZ re-sampling)". The lead sentence "Each challenger brings a hazard that the BQ25798 does not have" then rests on tTRANS_CHG alone for the BQ25773, which is unaffected.

4. Section 5.1 HR codes (line 578): after "FC bound after a POR 1.15 A, margin 0.05 A to the 1.20 A arm drop" insert "; after an IINDPM register loss the FC bound is conditional on P6 / S1-B6: 1.15 A if re-sampled, 1.70-1.76 A if sampled once".

Mirror: scoring.md:174-175 (observation three under Table 5) takes the same replacement as edit 2.

Proof of the corrected numbers: 1.55 + 0.15 = 1.70 A and 1.61 + 0.15 = 1.76 A from the dossier's own baseline band (dossier_BQ25798.md:276-277) and aux term (:264); 1.76 - 1.20 = 0.56 A; 1.76 - 1.40 = 0.36 A; 1.003 + 0.15 = 1.153 A reproduces the retained POR figure. The branch wording quotes SLUSDV2C p.27 verbatim. No score or rank changes (score.py has no HR4 term; S3 already 2 for the BQ25798), so no re-run of score.py is required. Fix class: documentation correction with an existing bench gate (S1-B6, charger_replacement_evaluation_2026-11-05.md:911 already records "whether the pin is sampled once or continuously"); no firmware test, pinmap, protocol, or coefficient follow-on.

### CIS-R1-07

**Finding.** BQ25773 delivers 3.04 A at an 8.4 V pack at the threshold floor (ICHG low corner of the 3.200 A setting); 'The shortfall is a setting choice. The 3.264 A setting closes it'; reason 4: 'A 3.264 A setting closes the BQ25773 gap; the BQ25690 gap cannot close without a regen ceiling above the HR4 hard-stop row.'

**Problem.** The target defines the delivered current with every tolerance at its low corner and the regen VINDPM at its floor (lines 125-127). At 8.4 V the BQ25773 is limited by its input ceiling, not by ICHG. [D773] 7.3 gives a regen ceiling band of 1.30-1.79 A; [D773] 11.3 puts the 8.4 V low-corner input at 24.8 W (1.32 A x 18.81 V), or 24.5 W with the leakage term (1.30 A). Delivered = 1.30 x 18.81 x 0.95 / 8.4 = 2.77 A (2.80 A at 1.32 A; 2.78 A at eta 0.955): 0.33 A short, not 0.06 A. Raising ICHG to 3.264 A changes nothing because the ceiling binds. A guaranteed 3.1 A needs a ceiling minimum of 3.1 x 8.4 / (0.95 x 18.81) = 1.457 A; with the +-0.244 A pin, divider and leakage band the maximum becomes about 1.95 A, and the constant-margin allowance falls to 0.757 V x 0.4 mF / 1.95 A - 59 us = about 96 us, below the 110-115 us pass value. The BQ25773 shortfall is therefore structural in exactly the BQ25690 sense. Exposure: silently wrong published numbers, and the rule of succession (sections 7 and 6.4 item 5 single out the BQ25690 for the shortfall 'which the operator must accept explicitly') does not hold.

**Verification (CONFIRMED, major).** Delivered current at an 8.4 V pack, with VINDPM low edge 18.81 V and eta 0.95 (dossier section 11.3 basis):
- Ceiling 1.32 A (no leakage): 1.32 x 18.81 = 24.83 W, x 0.95 / 8.4 = 2.808 A.
- Ceiling 1.30 A (with leakage): 24.45 W, giving 2.766 A.
- At eta 0.955: 2.78-2.82 A.
- Delivered = min(3.04 A ICHG, 2.77-2.81 A ceiling) = 2.77-2.81 A. The shortfall is 0.29-0.33 A, not the published 0.06 A.

A 3.264 A setting gives min(3.10, 2.77) = 2.77 A, so it changes nothing.

Required ceiling minimum for 3.1 A: 3.1 x 8.4 / (0.95 x 18.81) = 1.457 A. The nominal setting is then 1.457 + 0.200 (pin) + 0.029 (divider, 0.7 % x 1.68 V / 0.4) + 0.016 (leakage) = 1.702 A. The maximum is 1.702 + 0.245 = 1.947 A.

Hard-stop allowance on the dossier's own I x t basis, using the EVAL rows at 201-230 A.us:
- At 1.947 A: 201 / 1.947 = 103 us, up to 118 us at the 230 A.us end. The low end is below the 110 us bench pass.
- Cross-check at 1.79 A: 201 / 1.79 = 112.3 us, which matches the dossier's "about 112 us".
- The finding's 96 us comes from a different formula (0.757 V x 0.4 mF / I - 59 us) that is not traceable to the working documents. The direction holds and the magnitude differs.

The 1.947 A maximum is also 13 % above the 1.72 A hard-stop row.

**Severity rationale.** This is a silently wrong published number. The decision table states a 0.06 A shortfall, and the recomputed shortfall is 0.29-0.33 A, about 5x larger. The table also states a remedy (the 3.264 A setting) that is computably ineffective, because the ceiling term binds: min(3.10, 2.77) = 2.77 A.

The error also flips a qualitative differentiator the operator is asked to act on. Section 5.0 and the rule of succession (:1067-1068) single out the BQ25690 for a shortfall "which the operator must accept explicitly". In fact the BQ25773, which leads while T-5.HIZ is open, carries the same structural class: closing its gap pushes the ceiling maximum to about 1.95 A, which is 103 us of hard-stop allowance against the 110 us bench pass.

This is not critical. No kill path or back-feed is created, and the ceiling still limits the input. The weighted scores are unchanged, because no criterion scores delivered current (target :553-555). The BQ25798 recommendation stands, because the BQ25798 delivers 3.10 A with the reference-biased ceiling.

**Counter-review (REFINE, major).** Major confirmed. I defend my 96 us allowance figure against the verifier's 103 us. The verifier derived 103 us from the I x t product of the 1.72 A EVAL row (201 A us / 1.947 A); the R1-33 verdict in this same round shows that the EVAL 2.5 rows are constant-charge, not constant I x t (Q = (t + 59 us) x I = 302.7-303.8 uC for all three rows, reproducing the [D798] 129 us and 108 us baseline values), and that carrying the 1.72 A product to a higher current is non-conservative. On the EVAL-consistent model the allowance at the 1.947 A ceiling maximum is 302.8 uC / 1.947 A - 59 us = 96.5 us, so my figure is the traceable one; the verifier's 103 us is an overstatement of about 7 us in the non-conservative direction. Both values sit below the 110 us bench pass, so the conclusion is unchanged either way: a guaranteed 3.1 A at 8.4 V needs a ceiling minimum of 1.457 A, the +-0.245 A band gives a 1.947 A maximum (13 % above the 1.72 A row), and the BQ25773 shortfall is structural in the BQ25690 sense. The published 0.06 A shortfall is 5x low (true 0.29-0.33 A at min(3.04, 2.77-2.81)), and the published remedy (3.264 A setting) is computably ineffective because the ceiling binds. Fix item 2 should quote 96 us (or '96-103 us depending on model') rather than 103 us, and the Table 5.1 'Against' column must be re-based per S01.

**Agreed fix.** 1. **Table 5.1, BQ25773 row (:536).**
   - Column 3 becomes: "2.77 A: input-limited at the regen ceiling minimum (1.30 A x 18.81 V, eta 0.95); the ICHG low corner (3.04 A at the 3.200 A setting) does not bind".
   - Column 4 becomes: "**Not met** (-0.33 A); a higher CHARGE_CURRENT setting does not close it".

2. **Section 5.0 bullet (:550-551).** Replace it with: "**BQ25773.** The shortfall is structural. A guaranteed 3.1 A at an 8.4 V pack needs a regen ceiling minimum of 3.1 x 8.4 / (0.95 x 18.81) = 1.46 A (C). With the ±0.245 A pin, divider and leakage band of [D773] section 7.3, the maximum becomes about 1.95 A, above the 1.72 A hard-stop row, with an allowance of about 103 us against the 110 us bench pass (C)."

3. **Following paragraph (:555).** Change "a disadvantage of the BQ25690" to "a disadvantage of the BQ25690 and the BQ25773".

### CIS-R1-S01

**Finding.** "Two challengers do not reach 3.1 A at an 8.4 V pack at their low tolerance corner" ... "On this record the BQ25690 and the BQ25773 fail the brief's 3.1 A figure"; "The ICHG 2.50 A cap of critic item C2 is closed on the CCB for every part"; M4 sources its 3.1 A figure to "HR1 (regen capture; >= 23 W input at the threshold)".

**Problem.** The 3.1 A figure is not a requirement of the vehicle; it is the BQ25798 regen-image ICHG setting of [WD] (r1-10, H.7), which [WD] 1388-1389 and 2001 hold at 2.50 A "until T-E-11/T-B-19 pass" ("charge current 3.10 A accepted by the pack", [WD] 63), and which [EVAL] 8.1 item 6 and 8.2 leave open ("Sets the ICHG ceiling (2.50 A today, 2.83 A for HR1 at 23 W)"; "or whether HR1 is restated at about 20 W"). [D798] 403 and 532 carry this forward ("3.1 A needs the pack's maximum charge current"). The target then (a) promotes the setting to a pass/fail figure, (b) applies it at an 8.4 V pack, where 3.1 A is 26.0 W out and 28.3 W in at eta 0.92, which is 23 % above the HR1 pass condition (23 W; 2.52 A at 8.4 V) and 50 % above the 18.9 W peak of the defining 3 -> 1 m/s, 12 A brake, and (c) declares the 2.50 A pack-pending cap "closed on the CCB" while listing "Pack limit" as an open ruling in 8.1. The BQ25690 the target marks "Not met (-0.48 A)" delivers 23.9 W input (its own Table 5.1: HR1 PASS, +3.9 %), 22.0 W into the pack (16 % above the brake peak) and 2.97 A at the 7.4 V pack where the 2.83 A figure was derived. The CCB changes nothing about the pack's charge-current acceptance, so the C2 row of Table 9 is a category error (an EVM-era register image was never the binding limit). Exposure: a decision the operator would make differently; reason 4 of the recommendation and the succession-rule reason "it delivers 2.62 A, not 3.1 A" rest on a figure that the operator has not yet admitted and that exceeds the hard requirement's own definition; the BQ25690's regen HR1 input margin is understated as a failure.

**Verification (CONFIRMED, major).** Recomputed (scratch script, .venv_hil python -I): HR1 ICHG at 7.4 V, eta 0.91: 23 x 0.91 / 7.4 = 2.828 A ([EVAL] 2.83 A reproduced). HR1 pass condition expressed as charge current at 8.4 V, eta 0.92: 23 x 0.92 / 8.4 = 2.519 A. 3.1 A at 8.4 V = 26.04 W out, 26.04 / 0.92 = 28.30 W in = +23.1 % over 23 W and +49.8 % over the 18.9 W brake peak. BQ25690 at the ceiling minimum: 1.285 A x 18.62 V = 23.93 W in (+4.0 % vs 23 W), 23.93 x 0.92 = 22.01 W into the pack (+16.5 % vs 18.9 W), 22.01 / 8.4 = 2.62 A, 22.01 / 7.4 = 2.97 A (target and [D690] figures reproduced). Target's structural claim also reproduces: ceiling minimum for 3.1 A at 8.4 V = 3.1 x 8.4 / (0.92 x 18.62) = 1.520 A, x 1.715/1.285 = 2.03 A maximum > 1.72 A HR4 row; this arithmetic is correct but is a bound on a figure that is not the requirement. WD H.7 cross-check: 23 x 0.93 / 7.4 / 0.935 = 3.09 A (WD states 3.05 A; same order, 3.10 A chosen). BQ25773 at 3.04 A, 8.4 V = 25.5 W out vs the HR1 output-equivalent 23 x 0.92 = 21.2 W. Every "Not met" verdict in Table 5.1 sits 16-20 % above the HR1 pass condition in power.

**Severity rationale.** A published pass/fail verdict ("Not met", bold, Table 5.1; reason 4 of the recommendation; section 7 "which the operator must accept explicitly") is derived from a design setting that the authoritative requirement sources ([EVAL] 1.1, 3.3, 8.1) hold open and at a pack voltage (8.4 V) 13.5 % above the one where the figure was derived (7.4 V). Computed bound: the test is 23 % stricter in input power than HR1's own pass condition, and both parts marked "Not met" pass HR1 by +3.0 to +4.0 % on the target's own numbers. Exposure path: the rule of succession (target:1058-1068) tells the operator the BQ25690 lead "carries its 2.62 A delivered-current shortfall ... which the operator must accept explicitly" - a decision the operator would weigh differently knowing the part exceeds the hard requirement and the brake peak by 16 %. Second exposure: a claim the sources mark open (pack maximum charge current, [EVAL] 8.1 item 6, [WD] T-E-11) is stated as settled ("closed on the CCB", target:1032; [SCO]:278) while the same document lists it as an open ruling (:1086). Not critical: no kill path, back-feed or loop; the scored totals and the ranking are unchanged (target:554-555 "no criterion scores delivered current").

**Counter-review (DEFEND, major).** Major confirmed and I hold it. The 3.1 A figure is the BQ25798 regen-image ICHG setting ([WD] H.7, derived at a 7.4 V pack: 23 x 0.93 / 7.4 / 0.935 = 3.09 A), held at 2.50 A in [WD] until T-E-11/T-B-19 pass and left open by [EVAL] 8.1 item 6; the target promotes it to a bold pass/fail column and applies it at 8.4 V, where 3.1 A is 28.3 W input at eta 0.92, 23 % above the HR1 pass condition (23 W; 2.52 A at 8.4 V) and 50 % above the 18.9 W brake peak. Both parts marked 'Not met' pass HR1 by +3.0 to +4.0 % on the target's own numbers, and the BQ25690 puts 22.0 W into the pack, 16 % above the brake peak. The rule of succession then tells the operator to 'accept explicitly' a shortfall against a figure the operator has not admitted, while 'closed on the CCB' (:1032) sits beside the same item listed as an open ruling (:1086). That is a decision input the operator would weigh differently and a claim marked open by the authoritative sources stated as settled. Not critical: no kill path, scores unchanged. The six edits are accepted, and they are the frame into which R1-04, R1-07, R1-14 and R1-24 must be fitted.

**Agreed fix.** (1) Target:77-80, replace reason 4 with: "4. **Every finalist meets the HR1 input condition at the threshold floor** (>= 23 W; Table 5.1). The 3.1 A regen setting of the BQ25798 image ([WD] H.7) is a design choice conditional on the pack ruling; at an 8.4 V pack the BQ25690 (2.62 A, 23.9 W) and the BQ25773 (3.04 A, 23.7 W) deliver less than that setting but 16 % or more above the 18.9 W brake peak." (2) Target:123-127, replace "The regen path needs a charge current of 3.1 A or more" with "The regen path needs >= 23 W input at the threshold (HR1); the BQ25798 image sets ICHG 3.10 A for this at a 7.4 V pack ([WD] H.7: 23 x 0.93 / 7.4 with the -6.5 % ICHG tolerance), conditional on the pack's maximum charge current ([EVAL] section 8.1 item 6)." (3) Table 5.1 (:532-538): rename the column "Against 3.1 A" to "Against the 3.10 A BQ25798 image setting" and replace "**Not met**" with "Below the setting"; keep the HR1 column as the pass/fail column. (4) Target:553, replace "fail the brief's 3.1 A figure" with "deliver less than the BQ25798 3.10 A image setting at the low tolerance corner; all five meet the HR1 input condition". (5) Target:1032-1033 and [SCO]:278 (and the 6.6 reference): replace "closed on the CCB for every part" with "open for every part on the pack ruling ([EVAL] section 8.1 item 6; [WD] T-E-11); every part's ICHG range covers 3.1 A, so the CCB adds no limit". (6) Target:982 and :1067-1068, qualify "delivers 2.62 A, not 3.1 A" as "delivers 2.62 A at an 8.4 V pack, below the BQ25798 3.10 A image setting but 4.0 % above the HR1 input condition". Proof: each replacement number is the recomputation above (2.519 A HR1-equivalent at 8.4 V; 23.93 W / 22.01 W / 2.62 A / 2.97 A for the BQ25690; 28.30 W for 3.1 A at 8.4 V). Follow-on skill: none of test/pinmap-audit/protocol-bump/regen-coeffs/bench-incident; this is a documentation correction that also touches [SCO] Table 9 and section 8.5 (same wording at scoring.md:278 and :429).

### CIS-R1-S08

**Finding.** LTC4015 B: S4 threshold quality 3 (P), 8.00 points; Table 6.5 B1: LTC4015 49.8 (+10.7); statement 3: 'With the weights set to 0 (B1) the BQ25690 and the LTC4015 pass 10 points'.

**Problem.** [D4015] line 151 grades H4 as 'P range / B warm, taper, hold draw' and adds 'full-scale accuracy only; taper 1.2 -> 1.15 V typical only'. [D4015] line 282 gives the regen zero-current point as 18.13 V nominal and 17.92 V worst low against an 18.92 V setting. That is -4.2 % from the setpoint and below the 18.5 V bottom of the M8/S4 band, so the zero-current threshold does not meet the S4 anchor-2 accuracy (<= +-2 % at 18-19 V) on P evidence. The taper is B evidence, and section 1.4 caps B at 2. With S4 = 2 the LTC4015 B total is 46.0 (+5.7, not +8.3). B1 becomes (46.333 - 2.667 - 2.333) x 100/93 = 44.4 against 39.1, a margin of +5.4 rather than +10.7, which makes statement 3 false for the LTC4015. A1, A2 and A4 fall from +13.3 to +10.7. The ranking does not change, because the LTC4015 already fails condition 1, but the published totals and the sensitivity statements are wrong.

**Verification (PARTLY_CONFIRMED, major).** Taper (DS p.13, typical) at k = 15.766:
- Zero-current point: 1.15 x 15.766 = 18.13 V.
- Full-current point: 1.2 x 15.766 = 18.92 V.
- Offset: 18.13 / 18.92 = 0.958, which is -4.2 % (nominal).
- Worst low: 18.13 x (1 - 0.012) = 17.92 V. This is a C extrapolation, because the taper has no published tolerance.
- 17.92 V and 18.13 V both lie below the 18.5 V M8 regen floor.

The finding's own B1 (44.4, +5.4) is an arithmetic error; the correct value is 46.95 (+7.89). Rank order is unchanged: LTC4015 B stays fourth, below BQ25773 49.0 and BQ25690 48.3.

**Severity rationale.** This is the exposure class "silently wrong published numbers".

The LTC4015 B total moves from 48.7 to 46.0 (-2.67 points), and the published margin moves from +8.3 to +5.7. These figures appear in the summary table (line 60), Table 6.2, Table 6.3 and every LTC4015 cell of Table 6.5.

One published sensitivity statement becomes false. Statement 3 says the LTC4015 passes 10 points in B1, but the recomputed margin is +7.9.

The finding does not reach the other exposure classes. Ranking is unchanged: the LTC4015 was already fourth and already fails decision-rule condition 1 (M3 F, M5 c). The recommendation (keep the BQ25798) and the shortlist order are therefore unaffected. No kill path or loop is involved.

The severity is major, not critical, because the decision does not change.

**Counter-review (REFINE, major).** I accept the upgrade to major and the correction of my own B1 arithmetic (46.95 / +7.89, not 44.4 / +5.4). The zero-current point 1.15 x 15.766 = 18.13 V (worst low 17.92 V, C extrapolation) is -4.2 % from the setpoint and below the 18.5 V M8 floor, the taper is typical-only (B), and [CRIT] M8 defines the threshold as the zero-input-current point, so S4 = 3 (P) is not supportable; the alternative of scoring the full-current point is not. The scratch re-run moves LTC4015 B from 48.7 (+8.3) to 46.0 (+5.7) in the summary table, Tables 6.2, 6.3 and every Table 6.5 cell, drops A1/A2/A4 from +13.3 to +10.7, and makes statement 3 false (B1 +7.9, not above 10). Silently wrong published numbers in the headline table are the major class; not critical because the LTC4015 is fourth and fails condition 1 regardless, so the recommendation and order are unchanged. The ten edits plus [SCO] Tables 10/11/13, dossier_LTC4015 16.1 and the score.py LTC4015-A/B rows accepted; this is the second finding requiring a score.py re-run.

**Agreed fix.** Cap the LTC4015 S4 score at 2 (B) everywhere and propagate the change.

### 3.1 Residual disagreement

CIS-R1-07: the verifier computed the BQ25773 hard-stop allowance at a 1.947 A ceiling maximum as 103 us, from the I x t product of the 1.72 A [EVAL] row. The counter-review defended 96.5 us on the constant-charge model (Q = (t + 59 us) x I = 302.8 uC), which the CIS-R1-33 verification found consistent with all three [EVAL] section 2.5 rows. The adjudication adopts 96.5 us, because the constant-charge model reproduces the rows and the I x t extrapolation is non-conservative at a higher current. Both values lie below the 110 us bench pass.

## 5. All findings: verification and counter-review

### CIS-R1-01 (decision; reviewer major; final minor; accepted)

- Location: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:47-49 (headline), :1001-1003 (6.5 reason 3), :961-963 (statement 1), :228 (condition 4)
- Claim: "No candidate beats the BQ25798 by enough to justify a switch"; "Ten points equal two levels of S1 ... A switch is justified when a challenger is better by the equivalent of a documented warm-response advantage ... that no datasheet in the set provides."
- Problem: The switching test cannot be passed on any evidence outcome in which the baseline stays eligible. S1 is capped at 1 (U) or 2 (B) for every part because no datasheet publishes a warm response ([CRIT] 3.2); condition 4 pins S1 equal; scenario A3 (every part passes its bench gate, S1 = 2) leaves every margin unchanged at +8.0 / +8.7 / +8.3. The only scenarios that cross 10 either leave condition 1 open by the document's own grading (B1, D3, E2) or make the baseline ineligible (A2), where "the decision is no longer a scoring question" (statement 1). Reason 3 is therefore circular: the threshold is calibrated to the one criterion whose evidence cap forbids any part from earning it, and the headline presents a property of the rule as a measured result. Section 6.4 discloses that "HR1 decides the switch, through the BQ25798 bench gate", but section 0.1 does not. Exposure: the operator reads a 100-point scoring exercise as the basis of the decision when the decision is in fact made by the switching-cost argument (6.5 reason 1) and the S1-B4 gate alone.
- Reviewer recommendation: State in 0.1 that condition 2 is unreachable under the evidence caps while the baseline is eligible, that the retention rests on switching cost (6.5 reason 1) and that the switch question is decided by S1-B4. Either lower the margin to the switching-cost estimate alone or remove the S1-equivalence rationale (reason 3).
- Verdict: PARTLY_CONFIRMED, severity minor
- Evidence: Target and working documents (all paths under C:/Life Ops/School/Thesis/DC-Balancer-charger-search/docs/):

- charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:47-49 headline; :66-68 reason 1 ("A margin below about 10 points can reverse when these items close, without new bench data"); :81-82 reason 5; :88-89 condition S1-B4 ("If the BQ25798 warm-response bench gate fails, the BQ25798 fails M9 ... scenario A2"); :218-219 evidence cap; :223-231 decision rule (condition 4 is "S1 >= baseline S1", not "equal"); :931 condition 4 met at 1 = 1 for all; :944-957 Table 6.5; :961-963 statement 1; :1001-1003 reason 3; :1054-1056 section 7 S1-B4 wording.
- reviews/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10/screening_criteria.md:97 S1 anchors (3 requires "a published input-regulation loop bandwidth or step response"); :117-126 evidence cap (B caps 2, U caps 1); :73 M9; :81-82 note "No TI, ADI or MPS datasheet read in round 2 publishes one"; :154-166 decision rule.
- reviews/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10/scoring.md:72-79 strict S1 reading ("Every finalist and the baseline then score S1 = 1"); :295-312 Table 10 scores and evidence; :316-323 Table 11; :359-376 Table 13; :380-390 section 8.1; :451-470 section 9.
- reviews/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10/calc/score.py:5-12 score rows (reproduced in my scratch script).

What is CONFIRMED: (1) S1 is held at 1 (U) or 2 (B) for every part by the cap, and no S1 = 3 is reachable in the set (CRIT :97, :123; SCO :78). (2) Scenario A3 leaves margins at +8.0 / +8.7 / +8.3 (recomputed). (3) Every tabulated scenario crossing 10 either leaves condition 1 open/failed (B1, D3, E2 for BQ25690/LTC4015; A1, A4 for LTC4015) or makes the baseline ineligible (A2); the BQ25773, the only part meeting condition 1, never exceeds +9.3 (B1) with the baseline eligible. (4) Stronger than the finding states: the BQ25773 margin is a structural constant +8.67 under every symmetric closure of the open evidence, because all six criteria that separate it from the BQ25798 (S2, S3, S6, S9, S10, S15) already carry P/C evidence, and the five evidence-limited criteria (S1, S4, S5, S12, S13) are shared at equal scores. (5) A two-level S1 advantage (the unit reason 3 invokes) is unattainable while the baseline is eligible: challenger max 2, eligible baseline min 1 under the document's own A2 reading.

What is REFUTED or overstated: (a) "cannot be passed on any evidence outcome in which the baseline stays eligible" — an asymmetric bench outcome (BQ25773 gates B2/B4 pass, S1 -> 2; BQ25798 S1-B4 not yet run, S1 stays 1) yields +13.67 with condition 1 met and the baseline eligible; the document does not tabulate it, but it is a legal outcome of the rule. (b) "condition 4 pins S1 equal" — condition 4 is >=, and A4 (LTC4015 S1 read as 2) is a tabulated case of S1 inequality. (c) "section 0.1 does not [disclose]" — 0.1 lines 88-89 state the S1-B4 condition and reason 5 states that only a bench measurement resolves HR1. What 0.1 omits is narrower: switching cost is absent from its five reasons (it appears only at 6.5 reason 1 and CRIT 3.5), and 0.1 reason 1 implies the margin is evidence-live when, for the eligible challenger, it is invariant under symmetric closure.
- Quantification: Scratch script C:/Users/ricky/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/53f5790609a5edc5/e7982b37-24d6-4881-9fb4-c0e5262cc0ba/scratchpad/advrev/cis_r1_01.py (weights and scores transcribed from scoring.md Table 10 / score.py; run with the project venv Python).

Base totals reproduce: BQ25798 40.33; BQ25690 48.33 (+8.00); BQ25773 49.00 (+8.67); LTC4015 B 48.67 (+8.33); RAA489110 35.67 (-4.67).

BQ25773 - BQ25798 per criterion (weight x delta / 3): S2 +3.33 (P/P), S3 +2.67 (P/B), S6 +3.33 (PB/C), S9 +2.67 (P/P), S10 -2.33 (P/P), S15 -1.00 (C/C); sum +8.67. Shared evidence-limited criteria at equal score: S1 (1/1, U vs U/B), S4 (2/2, PB vs B), S5 (1/1, U/U), S12 (2/2, P vs P/U), S13 (1/1, cap/cap).

Symmetric closure (both parts lifted together): S1 -> 2: 45.33 vs 54.00, margin +8.67. S1, S5 -> 2: 47.67 vs 56.33, +8.67. S1, S5, S13 -> 2: 48.33 vs 57.00, +8.67. S1, S5 -> 2 and S12, S13 -> 3: 50.67 vs 59.33, +8.67. Invariant.

One S1 level = 15/3 = 5.00 points; two levels = 10.00 = the margin. Max S1 lead over an eligible baseline (S1 >= 1) = 2 - 1 = 1 level = 5.00 points, so reason 3's unit is unattainable while the baseline is eligible.

Asymmetric closure: BQ25773 S1 -> 2 with baseline untouched: 54.00 - 40.33 = +13.67 (condition 2 met, condition 1 met, baseline eligible). Once S1-B4 runs: pass -> 54.00 vs 45.33 = +8.67 (no switch); fail -> A2, baseline ineligible. BQ25773 with every U/B criterion at 2 and baseline untouched: 60.33 (+20.00) — the extreme asymmetric case.

Tabulated BQ25773 margins across Table 13 with the baseline eligible: max +9.3 (B1), min +3.7 (E1); no sign reversal in any scenario.
- Source: Working documents [CRIT] section 3.1 (S1 anchors), 3.2 (evidence cap), 3.5 (decision rule) and M9 note; [SCO] sections 2.2, 7.1 Table 10/11, 8 Table 13, 9; calc/score.py as the arithmetic authority for the scores. No datasheet claim is at issue; the finding is about the internal logic of the scoring rule, so the working documents and the recomputation settle it. The target's own prose (0.1, 6.4, 6.5, 7) was checked against them.
- Severity rationale: No published number is wrong (all totals and margins reproduce to 0.01), no hardware exposure path exists, and the recommendation, the shortlist order and the operator's next action (run bench gate S1-B4; rule on R-M2) are unchanged. The exposure is presentational: 0.1 reason 1 tells the operator that the margin "can reverse when these items close, without new bench data", whereas for the only condition-1-eligible challenger the margin is invariant at +8.67 under every symmetric evidence closure (bound computed above), and 6.5 reason 3 calibrates the threshold to a two-level S1 gap that cannot arise while the baseline is eligible (max one level = 5.00 points). An operator could therefore misdirect effort toward fetches and counts expecting them to decide the switch, when only S1-B4 (or strict R-M2) can. That is doc drift / a misleading rationale, not a decision the operator would make differently, so it caps at minor. The finding's own strongest claim (condition 2 unreachable on any eligible-baseline outcome) is refuted by the asymmetric bench case, which further limits severity.
- Minimal fix: Three wording changes to charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md; no calc or table changes.

(1) Section 0.1, reason 1 (lines 66-68): replace the last sentence "A margin below about 10 points can reverse when these items close, without new bench data." with: "These items can lower a challenger's margin (BQ25773: +7.0 under D1, +5.3 under D2, +3.7 under E1) but cannot raise it: every criterion that separates the BQ25773 from the BQ25798 (S2, S3, S6, S9, S10, S15) already rests on P or C evidence, and the open criteria (S1, S5, S12, S13) are shared at equal scores, so the margin stays at +8.7 whenever the two parts' evidence closes together. Condition 2 is reached by an eligible challenger only if the BQ25798 fails bench gate S1-B4 (condition S1-B4 below), or provisionally if a challenger passes its warm-response gate (+5.0 points; BQ25773 +13.7) before the BQ25798 gate is run."

(2) Section 0.1, add a sixth reason after reason 5 (or fold into reason 1): "6. **The margin covers the switching cost.** A switch discards the BQ25798 CCB layout in progress, six review rounds, the 112-entry test registry and the firmware register image (section 6.5, reason 1)."

(3) Section 6.5, reason 3 (lines 1001-1003): replace with: "3. **Scale of the margin.** Ten points equal two levels of S1, the heaviest criterion. No challenger can earn that gap through S1 itself while the BQ25798 is eligible: the evidence cap holds every S1 at 1 or 2, so the largest S1 lead over an eligible baseline is one level (5.0 points). The equivalence states the size of the margin; the route to it is the BQ25798 bench gate S1-B4 (section 6.4, statement 1)." Apply the same change to [SCO] section 9, third paragraph (scoring.md:465-467).

Proof: 15/3 = 5.00 points per S1 level; A3 and the three further symmetric closures give +8.67 each; D1/D2/E1 values are Table 6.5 rows reproduced by score.py; asymmetric S1 -> 2 gives 54.00 - 40.33 = +13.67. The decision rule, scores, and shortlist are untouched. Follow-on skill class: none (documentation edit; no test, pinmap, protocol, coefficient or bench action).
- Adjacent findings: Wording drift inside the target: 0.1 condition S1-B4 (line 88) reads "If the BQ25798 warm-response bench gate fails", while section 7 (line 1054) and [SCO] 10.2 read "fails while a challenger passes its equivalent gate"; the 0.1 version omits the challenger-pass clause that scenario A2 requires (challengers stay at S1 = 1 only if their gates have not failed).; [CRIT] internal inconsistency: the S1 score-0 anchor (screening_criteria.md:97) describes "a restart longer than 10 ms ... documented, but M9 passed conditionally", whereas M9 (line 73) says a documented restart of 10 ms or longer fails outright; no part can hold S1 = 0 and remain eligible, so scenarios A1/A2 use S1 = 0 for a bench outcome the anchor does not define.; 0.1 reason 1 says a sub-10 margin "can reverse" (change sign) when evidence closes; the minimum tabulated BQ25773 margin is +3.7 (E1) and the minimum for any TI challenger is +3.7, so no tabulated scenario reverses a margin — the verb overstates Table 6.5.; The untabulated asymmetric outcome (challenger warm-response gate passes before S1-B4 is run) is the only route to condition 2 with the baseline eligible, yet [SCO] 11.2 lists B2/B4 for the BQ25773 as deciding only "the drained-VCHG remedy and the CHG_QUAL gates"; their effect on S1 (1 -> 2, +5.0 points) is not recorded in the bench-gate table.
- Counter-review: REFINE (minor): I concede the strongest clause. The asymmetric case (BQ25773 S1 -> 2 before S1-B4 runs: 54.00 - 40.33 = +13.67) is a reachable outcome with the baseline eligible, so 'cannot be passed on any evidence outcome' is wrong as stated. The retained defect is narrower and the verifier's own enumeration proves it: for the only condition-1-eligible challenger the margin is invariant at +8.67 under every symmetric closure, and one S1 level (15/3 = 5.00) is the largest S1 lead over an eligible baseline, so reason 3 of 6.5 names a two-level gap no eligible challenger can earn by S1 itself. That is a misleading rationale in a recommendation reason, not a changed decision; the recommendation, shortlist order and the next action (S1-B4) stand. I accept minor and the three wording edits, with one addition: 0.1 should say in one sentence that the switch is decided by S1-B4 (or strict R-M2), which 6.4 statement 1 already says and 0.1 does not.

### CIS-R1-02 (decision; reviewer major; final minor; accepted)

- Location: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:50-51 and :63 (rank rule), :974-982 (statement 5), :1058-1065 (rule of succession); scoring.md:421-431
- Claim: "The rank follows the decision-rule outcome first and the weighted score second" (0.1). Rule of succession: "if T-5.HIZ passes, the BQ25690 leads ... and the BQ25773 is the alternate", on three prose reasons, with four counter-items listed after "However".
- Problem: Once T-5.HIZ passes both TI challengers meet condition 1, and the document's own second key (weighted score) orders BQ25773 49.0 above BQ25690 48.3. The succession rule replaces that key with "the hazard comparison of section 6.4 statement 5", an unweighted prose list in which three reasons for the BQ25690 are set against four admitted disadvantages (no ADC, /CE does not stop conversion, watchdog 40 s vs 0.5 s, 2.62 A not 3.1 A at an 8.4 V pack) with no stated weighing. Section 5.0 states that the BQ25690 shortfall is "structural" while the BQ25773 shortfall "is a setting choice"; the succession nevertheless promotes the BQ25690 to lead in both fallback cases (strict M2 and S1-B4). The [SCO] 8.5 amendment note says an earlier version "contradicted the ranking rule of section 10.1"; the amended text still does in the T-5.HIZ-pass branch. Of the three reasons, the first (1.10 A vs 1.39 A FC bound) rests on a nominal-vs-tolerance comparison. Exposure: the recommended part in a fallback the document itself declares plausible (R-M2 open, S1-B4 open) = a wasted board spin if the operator follows it.
- Reviewer recommendation: Either apply the stated second key (BQ25773 leads in both branches, BQ25690 alternate) or amend the rank rule in 0.1 and [SCO] 10.1 to a three-tier rule (condition 1, hazard comparison for totals inside the evidence band, weighted score) and state the hazard weights explicitly. In either case the 2.62 A structural shortfall (section 5.0) must be a scored or named veto item, not a trailing "However".
- Verdict: PARTLY_CONFIRMED, severity minor
- Evidence: Rank rule as stated: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:50-51 ("The rank follows the decision-rule outcome first and the weighted score second ([SCO] section 10.1)"); scoring.md:478-479 (same wording). Rule of succession: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:1058-1063 ("decision-rule condition 1 first and the hazard comparison of section 6.4 statement 5 second ... if T-5.HIZ passes, the BQ25690 leads"); restated without caveat at :91-92; statement 5 at :974-982 (three reasons, then "However" with four disadvantages, no weighing stated); scoring.md:421-431 (same, with the amendment note "an earlier version ... contradicted the ranking rule of section 10.1"). Decision rule: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:223-230 and screening_criteria.md:156-166 (condition 1 = must-haves; the second key is the weighted score only through the rank rule, not through [CRIT] 3.5, which defines alternates but no tie order). Evidence band: :63-64 ("score order among ranks 2-4 is not significant: the three totals lie within 0.7 points"), :998-1000 (unresolved evidence moves single parts 2.3-4.0 points). Structural vs setting choice: :546-551. The shortfall IS named as an explicit operator acceptance at :1067-1068 ("which the operator must accept explicitly"), so "trailing However" overstates it. Sub-claim "nominal-vs-tolerance": refuted by dossier_BQ25690.md:384-387,404 (FC bound = KILIM MAX 3780 / RILIM MIN 3980 ohm = 0.950 A, the HIGH tolerance corner, + 0.15 A aux = 1.10 A) and dossier_BQ25773_SMBus_twin_BQ25770G.md:301-309,545-553 (0.76-1.24 A = +-200 mA pin accuracy + divider + leakage band; 1.24 A is the HIGH corner, + 0.15 A = 1.39 A). Both figures are worst-case-high bounds. Open items that make the branch reachable: R-M2 (:1081, open), S1-B4 (:1092, open), T-5.HIZ (:1096, open); the BQ25773 ceiling position is itself an open ruling R-FC773 (scoring.md:534).
- Quantification: Weighted-score key: 49.0 - 48.3 = 0.7 points, inside the evidence band of 2.3-4.0 points per part (:998-1000), so the stated second key cannot order the pair significantly, as :63 itself says. BQ25690 delivered at the regen floor: 1.285 A x 18.62 V x 0.92 / 8.4 V = 2.621 A, shortfall 3.1 - 2.62 = 0.48 A (15.5 %). Structural: a 3.1 A guarantee needs a regen ceiling minimum of 3.1 x 8.4 / (0.92 x 18.62) = 1.520 A; scaled by the KILIM band 1.715 / 1.285 = 1.335 gives a maximum of 2.03 A > the 1.72 A HR4 hard-stop row, so the two cannot be met together (matches :546-549). BQ25773: 3.264 A setting at -5 % = 3.10 A, closes the shortfall with copper at 3.43 A (:550-551). FC bounds in reason 1: BQ25690 3780 / 3980 = 0.9497 A + 0.15 A = 1.0997 A (high corner, margin to the 1.20 A arm drop 0.10 A, about equal to the ~0.1 A INA253 zero-floor uncertainty cited at [D773]:558); BQ25773 1.24 + 0.15 = 1.39 A (high corner). Both are tolerance-corner figures: the "nominal-vs-tolerance" sub-claim is wrong. Watchdog ratio 40 s / 0.5 s = 80 (:981 correct).
- Source: Working documents (scoring.md 7.2, 8.5, 10.1, 11.1; screening_criteria.md 3.5; dossier_BQ25690.md 7.2-7.3; dossier_BQ25773 7.3, 11.4) and the target's own prose; no datasheet re-fetch was needed because the finding concerns the ranking logic, and the bounds it cites recompute from the dossier tables.
- Severity rationale: Reachability: the succession branch needs (R-M2 strict OR S1-B4 fail) AND T-5.HIZ pass, three items the document lists as open, so the branch is plausible but not the base case. Exposure: in that branch the rule names as lead a part whose delivered 2.62 A at an 8.4 V pack is 0.48 A (15.5 %) below the 3.1 A figure and structurally unfixable (needs a 2.03 A regen ceiling maximum against the 1.72 A HR4 row), over a part whose 0.06 A shortfall closes with a register setting; that is the "decision the operator would make differently" class. Capped at minor because (a) the recommendation (keep BQ25798) and Table 0.2 order are unchanged; (b) the 3.1 A delivered figure is not a must-have under the document's own criteria (M4 is a capability test, :543-544), so the rule does not select an ineligible part; (c) the shortfall is disclosed as an explicit operator acceptance at :1067-1068; (d) the reviewer's own remedy (apply the 0.7-point score key) is not defensible by the document's evidence band. The defect is an inconsistency between two stated rank rules, a documentation-class item.
- Minimal fix: Three edits, all documentation, no follow-on skill (no test, pinmap, protocol or coefficient change). (1) charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:50-51 and scoring.md:478-479, replace the sentence with: "The rank follows the decision-rule outcome first; where totals lie within the evidence band of section 6.5 (here 0.7 points against a 2.3-4.0 point band), the hazard comparison of section 6.4 statement 5 second; and the weighted score third ([SCO] section 10.1)." This makes the rule of succession at :1058-1059 an instance of the stated rule rather than a departure from it, and is consistent with :63. (2) After :982 (and scoring.md:429), add the weighing: "The three reasons are weighed above the four disadvantages because each concerns an HR1 or HR4 hazard with a hard-stop consequence (arm drop, burst-entry transition), whereas the delivered-current, ADC, /CE and watchdog items are margin notes under the criteria of section 1.3 (M4 is a capability test, section 5.0). The operator may reverse the order by ruling that the 3.1 A delivered figure at an 8.4 V pack is a hard requirement (ruling R-I3A1, Table 8.1)." Add row "R-I3A1 | Whether 3.1 A delivered at an 8.4 V pack is a hard requirement | Under a yes ruling the BQ25690 cannot lead in any branch (Table 5.1, section 5.0) and the BQ25773 leads after T-5.HIZ as well" to Table 8.1 (:1079 ff.) and to scoring.md 11.1. (3) :91-92, append "with its 2.62 A delivered-current shortfall accepted explicitly (section 7)". Proof: the corrected first key (condition 1) leaves both TI parts eligible after T-5.HIZ; the new second key is the one the text already applies; the third key (score) is never reached inside the band, so no rank in Table 0.2 or in the succession changes; the 2.62 A figure recomputes as 1.285 x 18.62 x 0.92 / 8.4 = 2.621 A.
- Adjacent findings: Reason 1 of statement 5 (:978) compares ceiling POSITIONS, not parts: the BQ25773's 1.39 A is at a ceiling position that is itself an open operator ruling R-FC773 (scoring.md:534); lowering its FC nominal to about 0.81 A puts the high corner + aux at the 1.20 A arm drop at the cost of a 1.01 A H5 low corner ([D773]:565-567), and the BQ25690 reference design also fails H5 at typical (1.46 A, [D690]:612). The real BQ25690 advantage is the narrower ceiling band (0.712-0.950 A, 0.24 A wide, KILIM +-13 %) against 0.76-1.24 A (0.48 A wide); the reason should be stated that way.; The BQ25690 FC-bound margin to the arm drop (0.10 A) is about equal to the INA253 zero-current floor uncertainty (~0.1 A, EVAL 2.3 as cited at [D773]:558); the BQ25798 dossier hedges its own 0.047 A margin as unverified until S1-B6 (dossier_BQ25798.md:264-266) but scoring.md:168 rates the BQ25690 figure P/C with no equivalent hedge.; The summary at :91-92 restates the succession without the 2.62 A caveat that :1067-1068 attaches to it; a reader of section 0 alone does not see the acceptance item.; scoring.md:429 cites 'Table 5' for the delivered-current figures while the target cites 'Table 5.1'; label drift only.
- Counter-review: REFINE (minor): My 'nominal-vs-tolerance' sub-claim was wrong: 3780/3980 + 0.15 = 1.10 A and 1.24 + 0.15 = 1.39 A are both high-corner figures. My remedy (apply the 0.7-point score key) is also not defensible inside a 2.3-4.0 point evidence band, as :63 itself says. What survives is the inconsistency between two stated rank rules and an unweighed 3-versus-4 prose comparison in the branch where the document names a lead; the verifier's bound (2.62 A is 0.48 A / 15.5 % under the 3.1 A figure and structurally unfixable, versus 0.06 A closable by a register) confirms the asymmetry but the figure itself is now shown by S01 to be a design setting, not the requirement, which further weakens my exposure claim. I accept minor and the three edits, including the R-I3A1 ruling row, which gives the operator the lever I was asking for.

### CIS-R1-03 (decision; reviewer major; final minor; accepted)

- Location: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:66-68 (reason 1), :998-1000 (6.5 reason 2), :940-957 (Table 6.5)
- Claim: "A margin below about 10 points can reverse when these items close, without new bench data."
- Problem: Table 6.5 contradicts the claim. Across all 13 scenarios the lowest margin of any leading challenger is +3.7 (BQ25773, E1) and +4.0 (BQ25690, E1); the LTC4015 margin never falls below +8.3 in any non-S1 scenario; no margin reverses sign. The scenarios that actually close evidence (C1, the stock and price fetches) move the margins to +9.0 / +8.0 / +9.3, i.e. up for two of three; the scenarios that lower margins (D1, D2, E1) are reading and counting choices, not evidence closures. The band is also asymmetric by construction: the U/UNVERIFIED cap at 1 falls on three challengers' S12 and every part's S13, so closing the fetches can only raise challengers relative to a baseline that already holds S12 = 2 on P evidence. The evidence band therefore argues at least as strongly that the true margin is above 10 as that it is below 8.
- Reviewer recommendation: Replace "can reverse" with the measured statement from Table 6.5 (margin range +3.7 to +13.7 across scenarios; sign never reverses; crosses 10 in B1, D3, E2 and the S1 scenarios). Drop the band as an argument for the direction of the margin and keep it only as an argument about rank order among the three challengers.
- Verdict: PARTLY_CONFIRMED, severity minor
- Evidence: Target claim: C:/Life Ops/School/Thesis/DC-Balancer-charger-search/docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:66-68 ("A margin below about 10 points can reverse when these items close, without new bench data") and :998-1000 (6.5 reason 2, same figures). ### CIS-R1-04 (decision,facts,coverage; reviewer major; final major; accepted)

- Location: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:69-72 (reason 2), :76-80 (reason 4), :534-536 (Table 5.1), :553-555 and :572-573 (section 5.0/5.1), :578 (5.1 HR codes), :628-630 (5.2), :848, :853, :857-859 (Table 6.0b)
- Claim: "The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound after a register loss exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop"; BQ25798 "1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal)" vs BQ25773 "1.39 A ... exceeds by 0.19 A (C)", "a hazard that the BQ25798 does not have". Table 5.1: BQ25798 "3.10 A with the reference-biased ceiling ... Met at the floor" vs BQ25690 "2.62 A at the ceiling minimum" and BQ25773 "3.04 A (ICHG low corner)" (not met).
- Problem: The two sides of each comparison are not computed to the same tolerance basis, although section 1.1 defines the delivered figure with every tolerance at its low corner. (a) Delivered charge: 3.10 A is the BQ25798 NOMINAL ICHG (dossier_BQ25798.md 11.3, 'ICHG-limited'); the input path is not limiting nominally (1.55 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4 = 3.18 A). SLUSDV2C p.11 (ICHG_ACC, buck, VBAT 8 V, TJ -40 to 85 C) gives -6.5/+3.5 % at 2 A and nothing above 2 A ([D798] 11.1); at that corner the BQ25798 delivers 3.1 x 0.935 = 2.90 A, 0.20 A short, a larger shortfall than the BQ25773's -0.06 A. The target applies the +3.5 % upper tolerance for headroom (line 248: 3.21 A), so the omission of the lower side is one-sided. The ILIM_HIZ pin-to-current conversion has no published accuracy ([D798] 7.1/7.2 item 3, B: S1-B6); an IINDPM-class -10 % on the 1.55 A floor gives about 1.40 A and 2.8-2.86 A delivered. The reference-biased 1.55-1.61 A band appears in no calc script (calc/bq798_ccb.py computes only the REGN divider). (b) FC bound: [D798] 7.2 item 1 grades 1.15 A 'C nominal: ideal Eq.1, no pin-to-current accuracy. The 0.047 A margin to the arm drop is smaller than any plausible conversion error and stays unverified until S1-B6'; a pin-conversion error worse than +4.7 % (0.047/1.003) puts the BQ25798 above 1.20 A. The BQ25773 1.39 A includes its published +-200 mA pin accuracy (SLUSEK7 EC p.12) and the BQ25690 1.285-1.715 A band includes the published KILIM spread. The 0.71-1.00 A FC band was computed for the REGN-referenced divider while the scored build ([SCO] Table 2) uses the reference-biased ceiling, and [D798] item 1 leaves open whether the FC leg is also reference-biased, so the FC band of the scored build is uncomputed. The BQ25773 POR bound is 0.15 A (CHARGE_CURRENT 0 at POR); its 1.39 A needs a register loss, which corresponds to a BQ25798 stray IINDPM write, not a POR. Consequently the "only part" claim in 6.1, headline reasons 2 and 4 and succession reason 1 compare a tolerance-free nominal baseline against worst-case corners for the challengers, and the "None" entry in the HR1 documented-contrary-fact row hides a B.
- Reviewer recommendation: State the BQ25798 as 'C nominal / U': 3.10 A at nominal ICHG, about 2.90 A at the 2 A-row tolerance extrapolated; or specify ICHG >= about 3.32 A so that min(3.32 x 0.935, 3.18) >= 3.10 A, with its copper basis, and mark above-2 A ICHG accuracy U. Compute the BQ25798 FC and regen bands with an assumed pin-to-current accuracy (state it, cite IINDPM accuracy as proxy, mark B) and compute the FC band for the scored reference-biased build; add the reference-biased band to a calc script. Label the BQ25798 1.15 A 'nominal, accuracy U (S1-B6)'. Strike "only part" and "Met at the floor" (or make them conditional) in Tables 5.1 and 6.0b; restate reason 2 (published tolerance vs unpublished, not a demonstrated absence of the hazard) and reason 4 symmetrically or drop it; re-examine succession reason 1.
- Verdict: PARTLY_CONFIRMED, severity major
- Evidence: VERDICT: PARTIAL. Part (a), the delivered-charge comparison, is CONFIRMED. Part (b), the FC bound, is only partly confirmed: the bases differ, but the target already labels the BQ25798 figure "C nominal", and the only published proxy does not support the finding's high-side error.

(a) Delivered charge.
- The target defines the delivered figure as "the worst-case current at an 8.4 V pack with every tolerance at its low corner and the regen VINDPM at its floor" (charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:125-127).
- The BQ25773 is scored on that basis: 3.200 A x (1 - 5 %) = 3.04 A ([D773] line 505; target :536, :78-79, :848).
- The BQ25798 3.10 A is the nominal ICHG setting. dossier_BQ25798.md:395-398 (section 11.3) labels it "(ICHG-limited)", and :399-400 computes only the input-ceiling side.
- The dossier itself records buck ICHG accuracy as "given only at 0.5-2 A (2 A: -6.5/+3.5 %, VBAT 8 V, DS EC p.11)" (dossier_BQ25798.md:373-374).
- The target uses the +3.5 % upper side for buck headroom: "3.21 A (3.10 A plus the +3.5 % ICHG tolerance)" (target :247-248). The lower side is never applied, so the treatment is one-sided.
- The target nonetheless publishes "Met at the floor" (:534), "the BQ25798 delivers 3.10 A with the reference-biased ceiling" (:79-80) and the same claim in Table 6.0b (:848).
- The reference-biased 1.55-1.61 A band exists only as prose in dossier_BQ25798.md:270-276 (section 7.2 item 3, "C, nominal Eq.1 only"). calc/bq798_ccb.py (57 lines) has no 2.5 V reference, 0.1 % ratio or 1.55/1.61 term.

(b) FC bound.
- The dossier grades the 1.15 A bound "C nominal: ideal Eq.1, no pin-to-current accuracy. The 0.047 A margin to the arm drop is smaller than any plausible conversion error and stays unverified until S1-B6" (dossier_BQ25798.md:262-264). The target carries the "C nominal" label at :578 and :853.
- The BQ25773 1.39 A includes its published +-200 mA pin accuracy and needs a partial register loss: IIN_HOST reset to 5 A while CHARGE_CURRENT stays set ([D773] lines 553-561).
- The finding is right that the comparison is unequal: one side is a published worst case, the other a tolerance-free nominal.
- The finding's high-side threat is not supported by the cited proxy. The only published proxy, IINDPM_ACC at 1 A, is 880-1000 mA, which has no positive error (dossier_BQ25798.md:247-248).
- The FC band of the scored reference-biased build is open, not computed: dossier item 1 (:265-267) leaves both options open ("Keep the [EVAL] FC band, or bias both legs from the reference").
- Therefore "only part whose FC bound ... exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop" (target :857-859) and "a hazard that the BQ25798 does not have" (:69-72) are stronger than the evidence. "Not demonstrated" is supported; "false" is not.
- Quantification: Delivered charge, BQ25798, reference-biased floor 1.55 A, VINDPM floor 18.62 V, efficiency 0.925 (dossier Fig 6-3 reading, 750 kHz):
- Input-limited current: 1.55 x 18.62 = 28.861 W; x 0.925 = 26.70 W; / 8.4 V = 3.178 A. The input path does not limit at nominal.
- ICHG low corner, 2 A-row -6.5 % applied as a percentage: 3.10 x 0.935 = 2.899 A. This is 0.20 A short of 3.1 A, more than the BQ25773 shortfall of 3.04 - 3.10 = -0.06 A.
- ICHG low corner, the 2 A-row error held at -130 mA absolute: 3.10 - 0.13 = 2.97 A, which is still short.
- Any negative ICHG error at a 3.10 A setting gives less than 3.10 A, so "Met at the floor" fails under the definition at :125-127 for any non-zero lower tolerance.
- Pin-conversion proxy of -10 % (B): 1.395 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4 = 2.860 A.

Setting that closes the ICHG side: S x 0.935 >= 3.10 gives S >= 3.3155 A, so 3.32 A in 10 mA steps, and 3.32 x 0.935 = 3.104 A. min(3.104, 3.178) = 3.104 A >= 3.10 A, at nominal pin conversion only.
- High corner: 3.32 x 1.035 = 3.436 A.
- Headroom term at 100 mOhm: 0.344 V instead of 0.321 V.
- Loss at 3.1 A is unchanged in class, because the input path limits at 3.18 A.

FC bound:
- 1.003 + 0.15 = 1.153 A, margin 0.047 A to 1.20 A.
- The error that consumes the margin is +4.7 % (0.047 / 1.003).
- The IINDPM proxy upper error at 1 A is 0 % (1000 mA maximum), so the margin holds under the proxy.
- The BQ25773 pin-accuracy class, +-200 mA at about 1 A (about +-20 %), would give 1.20 + 0.15 = 1.35 A on the BQ25798. This is unknown, not shown.

BQ25773 at POR: CHARGE_CURRENT 0, so the bus draw is 0.15 A aux only. The 1.39 A needs a partial register loss.
- Source: The dossier is a working document under the authority order: dossier_BQ25798.md sections 7.1, 7.2 and 11.1-11.3 (citing SLUSDV2C EC p.11, p.12 and Eq.1 p.27), and dossier_BQ25773 sections 11.1 and 11.4 (citing SLUSEK7 EC p.12). The target's own basis definition is at :125-127, and its use of the +3.5 % upper tolerance at :247-248. calc/bq798_ccb.py was inspected and contains no reference-biased band. The primary SLUSDV2C p.11 ICHG_ACC row was not re-fetched in this session, because no fetch tool was available. The -6.5/+3.5 % figure is taken from the dossier's citation.
- Severity rationale: The exposure class is silently wrong published numbers. Under the document's own stated basis (:125-127), the BQ25798 delivers no more than 2.97 A (absolute-error extrapolation) or 2.90 A (percentage extrapolation). It is published as "3.10 A ... Met at the floor" in three places, and that claim supports headline reason 4, which says only the challengers miss 3.1 A. The computed bound is a 0.20 A shortfall against a 0.06 A shortfall for the BQ25773, so the ranking inside reason 4 inverts between those two parts.

The finding does not reach critical, and it does not change the recommendation:
- No criterion scores delivered current (target :553-555), so the totals and the order do not move.
- The BQ25798 closes its gap with a register setting, as the BQ25773 does.
- The BQ25690's structural shortfall remains.

Part (b) is a basis inconsistency only. The target discloses "C nominal", and the published proxy gives no positive error, so no exposure path to the arm drop is shown. Alone, part (b) would cap at minor.
- Minimal fix: 1. Table 5.1 (:534), BQ25798 row, "Delivered at the threshold floor" cell. Replace with: "3.10 A at the nominal ICHG (input-ceiling limit 3.18 A with the reference-biased ceiling); 2.90 A at the ICHG low corner if the 2 A-row -6.5 % applies at 3.1 A (U: no ICHG accuracy is published above 2 A); 2.86 A with a -10 % pin error (B, S1-B6); an ICHG setting of 3.32 A gives 3.10 A at the low corner". In the "Against 3.1 A" cell, replace "Met at the floor" with "Met at nominal; not met at the extrapolated low corner (-0.20 A) unless ICHG is set to 3.32 A".

2. Section 0.1 reason 4 (:76-80). Replace "Two challengers do not reach 3.1 A ... the BQ25798 delivers 3.10 A with the reference-biased ceiling" with: "At the low tolerance corner the BQ25690 delivers 2.62 A (input-limited at its ceiling minimum). The BQ25773 delivers 3.04 A at its 3.200 A setting and the BQ25798 delivers about 2.90 A at its 3.10 A setting (2 A-row ICHG tolerance extrapolated, U). Setting changes close both gaps (BQ25773 3.264 A, BQ25798 3.32 A). The BQ25690 gap cannot close without a regen ceiling above the HR4 hard-stop row."

3. Make the same change in section 5.1 (:571-572), Table 6.0b row :848 and the summary at :553-555. The summary should name the BQ25690 as the only structural shortfall.

4. Section 2.2 (:248). If the 3.32 A setting is adopted, change "3.21 A (3.10 A plus the +3.5 % ICHG tolerance)" to "3.44 A (3.32 A plus +3.5 %)" and recompute section 2.3 with 0.344 V.

5. FC bound, at :578, :853, :69-72 and :857-859:
   - Replace "1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal)" with "1.15 A nominal, margin 0.05 A; pin-to-current accuracy unpublished (U, S1-B6); the IINDPM_ACC proxy at 1 A (880-1000 mA) adds no high-side error (B)".
   - Replace ":857-859 The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound ... exceeds" with "The BQ25773 is the only part with a published FC bound above the 1.20 A arm drop; the BQ25798 bound is nominal until S1-B6".
   - Reason 2 (:69-72): change "a hazard that the BQ25798 does not have" to "a documented hazard; the corresponding BQ25798 figure is nominal (S1-B6)".
   - Set the HR1 "documented contrary fact" cell for the BQ25798 to "None documented (above-2 A ICHG accuracy U)".

6. Calc record: add the reference-biased band (2.5 V +-0.5 %, 0.1 % ratio, 5 kOhm Thevenin, +-1.5 uA leakage) and the 3.32 A setting check to calc/bq798_ccb.py. The check is min(S x 0.935, 1.55 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4) >= 3.10.

Proof: 3.32 x 0.935 = 3.104 A >= 3.10 A, and 3.178 A >= 3.104 A, so the input path is not binding. The fix is a document correction. The follow-on skill is test in the calc-script sense (pin the band in bq798_ccb.py). No firmware, protocol or coeffs regeneration is triggered.
- Adjacent findings: Table 6.0b row :853 is headed 'after a charger POR or a register loss', but the BQ25798 entry gives only the POR / pin-ceiling bound. dossier_BQ25798.md:267-269 states that the pin ceiling itself is register-defeatable through REG14[1] (EN_EXTILIM), which leaves only IINDPM, up to 3.3 A. The BQ25773's analogous never-write fields are counted elsewhere, so this row should compare like with like or say 'pin ceiling intact' for both parts.; BQ25773 POR bound is 0.15 A (CHARGE_CURRENT 0 at POR). The 1.39 A in :76-77 and :628-630 needs IIN_HOST reset with CHARGE_CURRENT retained; the target's 'after a loss of its input-current register' wording is correct, but :853's heading pairs it with POR.; Efficiency 0.925 used for the BQ25798 input-limited figure is a typical figure reading at 20 V (dossier 11.2), not a low corner. Under the :125-127 basis, efficiency is also not at its low corner for any part. This is consistent across parts but contradicts 'every tolerance'.; The 1.5 MHz option (eta about 0.91) gives an input-limited 3.127 A, still above 3.10 A at nominal pin conversion; the 3.32 A fix holds at both switching frequencies only at nominal pin conversion.
- Counter-review: REFINE (major): Major is upheld on part (a) and I accept the verifier's narrowing of part (b). (a): under the document's own basis (:125-127, every tolerance at its low corner) the BQ25798 delivers min(3.10 x 0.935 = 2.90 A, 1.55 x 18.62 x 0.925 / 8.4 = 3.18 A) = 2.90 A, or 2.97 A on the absolute -130 mA reading; any non-zero lower tolerance makes 'Met at the floor' false, and the 0.20 A shortfall exceeds the BQ25773's published 0.06 A, inverting the comparison inside reason 4. The 3.32 A setting that closes it (3.32 x 0.935 = 3.104 A) is the symmetric remedy the document grants the BQ25773 and withholds from itself. (b): the IINDPM proxy at 1 A has no high-side error, so no exposure path to the arm drop is shown; the BQ25798 1.15 A is 'C nominal' as disclosed and the 'None' contrary-fact cell should read 'None documented (above-2 A ICHG accuracy U)'. Refinement: this finding must be edited jointly with S01 and R1-07. S01 shows 3.1 A is the BQ25798 image setting, not the requirement, so the corrected Table 5.1 column should be 'against the 3.10 A image setting' with all three low-corner figures (2.90 A U extrapolated, 2.77 A, 2.62 A) and HR1 as the pass/fail column; otherwise fixing the BQ25798 cell alone re-creates a one-sided table.

### CIS-R1-05 (facts; reviewer major; final minor; accepted)

- Location: docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:69-70 (section 0.1 reason 2), :631-633 (section 5.2), :849 (Table 6.1 'documented contrary fact')
- Claim: The BQ25773 'needs a 5 ms charge transition (tTRANS_CHG) ... longer than the 4 ms warm budget'; 'the 5 ms tTRANS_CHG, which alone exceeds the 4 ms warm budget'.
- Problem: SLUSEK7 section 6.6 p.21 lists tTRANS_CHG as 'Charge current transit from pre-charge level to fast-charge level time duration', 5 ms in the NOM column, with no MIN or MAX: a typical total ramp duration, not a time constant and not a guaranteed figure. The HR1 budget is tau_warm <= 4 ms, with 10-90 % <= 9 ms (line 128). If the 5 ms transit is a ramp from IPRECHG 0.384 A to 3.2 A, 63 % of 3.2 A (2.02 A) is reached at (2.02 - 0.384) / 2.816 x 5 = 2.9 ms, and 10-90 % takes less than 5 ms; both pass. Only the 2 ms board-only case fails. Calling it a documented contrary fact that 'alone exceeds' the budget is not supported; the waveform shape is unpublished, so the status is B. Exposure: one of the five recommendation reasons and the BQ25773's HR1 'contrary fact', while the BQ25773 leads the rule of succession.
- Reviewer recommendation: Reword as: 'a nominal 5 ms pre-charge-to-fast-charge transit (typical, shape unpublished); against tau <= 4 ms it passes if linear (63 % at about 2.9 ms) and fails the 2 ms board-only case; B on gate B4'. Remove it from the 'documented contrary fact' column, or mark it B.
- Verdict: CONFIRMED, severity minor
- Evidence: PRIMARY: SLUSEK7 (BQ25773, September 2024) section 6.6 Timing Requirements, p.21 (downloaded to C:\Users\ricky\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\53f5790609a5edc5\e7982b37-24d6-4881-9fb4-c0e5262cc0ba\scratchpad\advrev\dl_slusek7\bq25773.pdf): row tTRANS_CHG, "Charge current transit from pre-charge level to fast-charge level time duration", MIN blank, NOM 5, MAX blank, unit ms. No shape, no time constant, no guaranteed bound. Figure 7-4 p.32 draws the pre-charge-to-CC transition as an idealised vertical step and gives no shape either.
