Target restates this at charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:128.

TARGET CLAIMS: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:69-70 ("needs a 5 ms charge transition (tTRANS_CHG) ... longer than the 4 ms warm budget"), :631-632 ("needs the 5 ms tTRANS_CHG, which alone exceeds the 4 ms warm budget"), :849 (Table 6.0b "HR1 documented contrary fact": "tTRANS_CHG 5 ms > 4 ms budget"), :857-858 ("Three challengers carry a documented HR1 hazard"). Origin: docs/reviews/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10/dossier_BQ25773_SMBus_twin_BQ25770G.md:394-397 ("That transition alone exceeds the tau_warm <= 4 ms budget"), introduced by review item 12 at :826; the same dossier already codes the item as B/U (:146 HR1 "U (B: ... burst entry through pre-charge)", :157 H7 "B", :411 "S1 evidence: U"). scoring.md:427 repeats it as the third reason for preferring the BQ25690 when T-5.HIZ passes.

SCORE IMPACT: scoring.md:72-78 (strict S1 anchor: every finalist and the baseline score S1 = 1), :297 (S1 row, all 1), :346 (condition 4 met 1 = 1), :271 (the drained-VCHG scenario is booked as "equivalent open" for every part); calc/score.py:1 W S1 = 15 but no part's S1 input depends on tTRANS_CHG. dossier_BQ25798.md:312-313: the same drained-VCHG scenario pays the IC-independent D-BT-SQ RT1987 drop-out tD_ON 8 ms + tON about 1.9 ms for every candidate.
- Quantification: Setting 3.200 A (recommended), IPRECHG 0.384 A, span 2.816 A, nominal transit 5 ms.
Linear ramp: t63 = (0.63 x 3.200 - 0.384)/2.816 x 5 ms = 1.632/2.816 x 5 = 2.90 ms (< 4 ms: pass; > 2 ms board-only: fail). t10 = 0 (0.32 A < IPRECHG). t90 = (2.880 - 0.384)/2.816 x 5 = 2.496/2.816 x 5 = 4.43 ms, so 10-90 % = 4.43 ms (< 9 ms: pass).
Exponential reading (5 ms = settle to 95 %, 3 tau): tau = 1.67 ms, passes 4 ms and 2 ms; to 99 % (4.6 tau): tau = 1.09 ms.
Pure-delay reading (hold at 0.384 A for 5 ms, then step): t63 = 5 ms, fails 4 ms by 1.0 ms and the governing 4.5 ms table value by 0.5 ms.
So the claim "alone exceeds the 4 ms budget" holds only under the most pessimistic unpublished shape; the finding's 2.9 ms recomputation is correct. All readings fail the 2 ms board-only case except the exponential one.
Context bound: in the same drained-VCHG scenario every candidate pays the D-BT-SQ drop-out 8 + 1.9 = 9.9 ms (dossier_BQ25798.md:312-313), 2.5x the 4 ms budget; whether the 5 ms transit is serial (14.9 ms) or hidden under it (9.9 ms), the RT1987 term is the binding one, not the BQ25773 transit.
Score: S1 = 1 for every part under the strict anchor; removing or re-coding the tTRANS_CHG item changes no S-input, so totals (40.3 / 49.0 / 48.3 / 48.7 / 35.7) and margins are unchanged.
- Source: Primary datasheet SLUSEK7 section 6.6 p.21 (NOM-only transit duration) settles the premise; [EVAL] section 3.2 lines 558-567 settles the budget definition (tau = time to 63 %); scoring.md sections 2.2 and Table 5 plus calc/score.py settle the score impact (none); dossier_BQ25798.md section 8 settles the shared RT1987 drop-out context.
- Severity rationale: A B-status item (shape unpublished, bench gate B4 open) is stated as a documented datasheet failure in three places, including one of the five recommendation reasons and the Table 6.0b "documented contrary fact" cell. Exposure path: a reader takes the BQ25773's HR1 as datasheet-failed where the datasheet gives a nominal duration that passes the budget under a linear reading (2.90 ms) and fails only under a delay reading (5 ms). No computed bound moves: S1 = 1 for every part regardless (scoring.md:72-78, :297), so no score, margin, rank or the rule of succession changes (the BQ25773 already leads when the baseline fails; the BQ25690-preferred branch rests on two other reasons as well). No kill path, loop or back-feed is involved. Not major: the published number 5 ms is transcribed correctly and no stability or protocol claim depends on it; the defect is an unsupported inference presented as settled, which is the minor class (superseded/overstated claim stated as current).
- Minimal fix: Three text edits in docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md (and the mirrored sentence in scoring.md:427); no score or calc change.

1. Section 0.1 reason 2, lines 69-71, replace "The BQ25773 needs a 5 ms charge transition (tTRANS_CHG) on burst entry from a drained VCHG node, longer than the 4 ms warm budget, and its fuel-cell (FC) bus draw" with: "The BQ25773 passes through a nominal 5 ms pre-charge-to-fast-charge transit (tTRANS_CHG; SLUSEK7 section 6.6 p.21, NOM only, shape unpublished) on burst entry from a drained VCHG node; against tau_warm <= 4 ms it passes as a linear ramp (63 % at about 2.9 ms) and fails as a delay, so it is B on gate B4. Its fuel-cell (FC) bus draw".

2. Section 5.2, lines 631-633, replace the bullet with: "**Burst entry from a drained VCHG.** Behind D-BT-SQ the next burst starts in pre-charge and passes through the nominal 5 ms tTRANS_CHG (NOM only, no MIN or MAX, shape unpublished; SLUSEK7 section 6.6 p.21). As a linear ramp from IPRECHG 0.384 A to the 3.200 A setting, 63 % is reached at about 2.9 ms and 90 % at about 4.4 ms, inside the 4 ms and 9 ms budgets; as a delay it fails by 1.0 ms; every reading except an exponential one fails the 2 ms board-only case. The same scenario pays the IC-independent D-BT-SQ drop-out (tD_ON 8 ms + tON about 1.9 ms) for every candidate ([D798] section 8). A remedy (BATFETOFF_HIZ = 1, EN_LDO = 0, or a keep-alive above about 6.8 V) must be proven on gate B4 ([D773] section 8.3)."

3. Line 857-858: "Three challengers carry a documented HR1 hazard" -> "The BQ25690 and the LTC4015 carry a documented HR1 hazard that the BQ25798 does not carry; the BQ25773 item is a bench gate".

### CIS-R1-06 (system; reviewer major; final major; accepted)

- Location: docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:853,859 (Table 6.0b HR4 FC row and following sentence); section 0.1 reason 2 (lines 69-71); section 5.1 HR codes (line 578)
- Claim: BQ25798 FC bus draw after a charger POR or a register loss is 1.15 A (margin 0.05 A to the 1.20 A arm drop). The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound after a register loss exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop and the 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX, a hazard the BQ25798 does not have.
- Problem: The 1.15 A figure assumes ILIM_HIZ is re-sampled continuously. That is P6, which is open (B, S1-B6). [EVAL] line 655 states the bad branch: 'If only at qualification, a Run-entry qualification in regen mode keeps the regen clamp through an FC window (the FC bound is then IINDPM, written and read back, plus firmware)'. The branch is reachable under the always-powered design, because the charger qualifies at Run entry with REGEN closed on the regen leg. In that branch the BQ25798's hardware FC bound is the regen ceiling, 1.55-1.61 A ([D798] 7.2): above the arm drop, above P_MAX and above OC_FC 1.4 A, and larger than the 1.39 A held against the BQ25773. After a register loss (IINDPM), the BQ25798 is then in the same hazard class as the BQ25773, and worse. Table 6.0b grades the BQ25798 row 'C nominal' without the P6 condition, and reason 2 counts the hazard against the BQ25773 alone.
- Reviewer recommendation: Mark the BQ25798 HR4 FC row as conditional on S1-B6/P6, with the bad-branch bound of 1.55-1.61 A after an IINDPM loss. Remove 'the only part' from line 859 and from reason 2 of section 0.1, or restate them as conditional on S1-B6.
- Verdict: CONFIRMED, severity major
- Evidence: Target claims: C:/Life Ops/School/Thesis/DC-Balancer-charger-search/docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:853 (Table 6.0b BQ25798 cell "1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal)" under the row heading "after a charger POR or a register loss"), :859 ("The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound after a register loss exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop and the 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX"), :69-71 (reason 2 counts the 1.39 A register-loss bound against the BQ25773 as a hazard "the BQ25798 does not have"), :578 ("FC bound after a POR 1.15 A, margin 0.05 A"). None of these cells or sentences carries the P6 condition; P6 is named only as a generic open item at :587 and :1030.

Source of the 1.15 A figure: docs/reviews/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10/dossier_BQ25798.md:264-267 ("FC bound after a charger POR: <= 1.003 A + 0.15 A aux = 1.15 A ... stays unverified until S1-B6"). The dossier's own HR4 row conditions it: dossier_BQ25798.md:155 ("re-sampling after qualification is P6 / S1-B6"); dossier_BQ25798.md:244-247 states the pin is read "after PG_STAT at each qualification" and that continuous following "is the P6 contradiction ([EVAL] 0.3), bench S1-B6". scoring.md:274 lists P6 as "Open (S1-B6)" for the BQ25798 and closed for every challenger; scoring.md:174-175 carries the same unconditional "only part" sentence.

Bad branch stated by the prior design: docs/charger_replacement_evaluation_2026-11-05.md:653-656 ("If only at qualification, a Run-entry qualification in regen mode keeps the regen clamp through an FC window (the FC bound is then IINDPM, written and read back, plus firmware)"); the same file :215 (P6 row, "ILIM_HIZ may be sampled only at qualification").

Reachability of the branch under the designed sequence: charger_replacement_evaluation_2026-11-05.md:733 (Run entry: "P5 LOW; CHG_QUAL HIGH; REGEN HIGH ... CHG_QUAL LOW; write and read back the regen image") - the qualification read happens with REGEN closed and FCC LOW, so Q3 is off and the divider is in its regen position (EVAL :649-650, dossier_BQ25798.md:257-260); :734 (Regen -> FC transition: REGEN LOW then FC_CHARGE HIGH, "no unplug: VCHG-IN 70 uF falls at ~40-70 V/s, far above VBUS_PRESENTZ", then "write + read back FC image") - no re-qualification occurs, so a once-sampled clamp stays at the regen value through the FC window.

Regen clamp band taken as the dossier baseline: dossier_BQ25798.md:276-282 (reference-biased option "about 1.55-1.61 A"; "This dossier takes the reference-biased ceiling as the baseline"); REGN-divider alternative 1.42-1.81 A at :259.

Comparator for the BQ25773: dossier_BQ25773_SMBus_twin_BQ25770G.md:553-556 ("IIN_HOST lost (POR value 5 A) | ceiling <= 1.24 A; + 0.15 A aux = 1.39 A"), i.e. the same aux-inclusive construction.

Primary datasheet (local copy, read only): BQ25798_SLUSDV2C.pdf p.27 section 7.3.4.3 "At POR, before the charger converter starts switching, the charger ADC reads the ILIM_HIZ pin voltage ... The ILIM_HIZ pin sets a high clamp for the IINDPM register. If the IINDPM setting from the D+/D- detection or the POR default 3A IINDPM setting is higher than the ILIM clamp, the IINDPM register stays at this ILIM clamp. In addition, the host cannot program the IINDPM register to any values higher than this ILIM clamp after POR, unless the register bit EN_EXTILIM is set to 0"; p.26 section 7.3.4 step 6 (ADC reads ILIM_HIZ after PG_STAT at qualification); p.5 pin table ("the lower setting of ILIM_HIZ pin and the IINDPM register"); p.63 Table 7-18 (REG06 IINDPM POR 3000 mA, "Reset by: REG_RST" only). No datasheet passage describes a continuous re-read of the pin; the only described mechanism is the ADC read at POR/qualification with the result held as a clamp.
- Quantification: Good branch (ILIM_HIZ re-sampled after Q3 switches, FC divider in force): 1.003 A (FC band maximum, dossier 7.2) + 0.15 A aux = 1.153 A; margin to the 1.20 A arm drop 0.047 A. This reproduces the target's 1.15 A / 0.05 A.

Bad branch (sampled once, at the regen Run-entry qualification; IINDPM register lost so the effective limit falls back to the held clamp): reference-biased clamp 1.55-1.61 A + 0.15 A aux = 1.70-1.76 A bus draw in an FC window.
- vs 1.20 A arm drop: exceeds by 0.50-0.56 A (the BQ25773 exceeds by 0.19 A).
- vs 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX: exceeds by 0.45-0.51 A.
- vs 1.4 A OC_FC raw-sample latch: exceeds by 0.30-0.36 A, so ERR_OC_FC fires (fault path intact).
- vs the 1.39 A held against the BQ25773: larger by 0.31-0.37 A.
With the REGN-divider option instead (1.42-1.81 A): up to 1.81 + 0.15 = 1.96 A.

The finding's own figure "1.55-1.61 A" omits the 0.15 A aux that both the target's 1.15 A and the BQ25773's 1.39 A include; the like-for-like bound is 1.70-1.76 A.

Register-loss paths for the BQ25798 IINDPM: REG_RST, a stray write to REG06, or EN_EXTILIM = 0 (REG14[1]); the watchdog does NOT reset REG06 (Table 7-18 p.63), unlike ICHG (Table 7-16 p.61). After a REG_RST the POR value 3000 mA lands at the held clamp per section 7.3.4.3.

Scoring impact: zero. score.py carries no HR4/FC-bound term; S3 already scores the BQ25798 at 2 (B) citing P6 (dossier_BQ25798.md:586, [CRIT] S3 band 3 requires continuous re-sampling). The 40.3 / 49.0 totals and the 8.7-point margin are unchanged.
- Source: Primary datasheet BQ25798 SLUSDV2C (revised June 2026) pp.5, 26-27, 61, 63 settled the mechanism (ADC read at POR/qualification held as an IINDPM clamp; REG06 reset by REG_RST only). [EVAL] docs/charger_replacement_evaluation_2026-11-05.md:653-656, :733-734 settled reachability (Run-entry qualification in the regen divider position; no re-qualification on Regen -> FC). Working documents dossier_BQ25798.md:155, :244-282 and scoring.md:274 settled that P6 is open and that the 1.55-1.61 A / 0.15 A / 1.003 A inputs are the dossier's own baseline. dossier_BQ25773_SMBus_twin_BQ25770G.md:553-556 settled the aux-inclusive construction of the 1.39 A comparator.
- Severity rationale: Computed bound: 1.70-1.76 A hardware FC-window bound after an IINDPM loss in the sampled-once branch, against the 1.15 A published, exceeding the 1.20 A arm drop by 0.56 A and the OC_FC latch by 0.36 A, and 0.31-0.37 A above the 1.39 A the document holds against the BQ25773. Reachability: the designed Run-entry sequence qualifies in the regen divider position and the Regen -> FC transition never re-qualifies, so the branch is the normal operating path whenever the datasheet-described sampled-once behaviour holds; the datasheet describes only that behaviour (section 7.3.4.3 "sets a high clamp ... after POR"), so the branch is at least as likely as the one the target assumes. Exposure class: a silently wrong published comparative number in one of the five stated reasons for the recommendation (reason 2) and in the HR table a reader would use to rank the parts; the hazard is counted against the BQ25773 alone while the BQ25798 shares it at a larger magnitude. Not critical: the OC_FC raw-sample latch (1.4 A) fires before any converter-kill or back-feed path, and the H-20 excursion above P_MAX is bounded to the latch time. Does not flip the recommendation: the score carries no HR4 term, S3 already discounts P6, and the BQ25773's tTRANS_CHG hazard stands on its own; the operator would, however, read reason 2 differently.
- Minimal fix: Four text edits in docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md, plus the mirror sentence in the working document.

1. Table 6.0b (line 853), BQ25798 cell of the row "HR4 FC bus draw after a charger POR or a register loss (limit: 1.20 A arm drop)": replace "1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal)" with "After a POR: 1.15 A, margin 0.05 A (C nominal). After an IINDPM loss: 1.15 A only if ILIM_HIZ is re-sampled after qualification (P6, S1-B6 open); if sampled once, the regen clamp latched at the Run-entry qualification persists through FC windows and the bound is 1.70-1.76 A (reference-biased 1.55-1.61 A + 0.15 A aux), above the arm drop by 0.56 A and the 1.4 A OC_FC latch by 0.36 A ([EVAL] 4.1; [D798] 7.2)". Row "HR4 status" (line 854): "C nominal" -> "C nominal (regen); conditional on S1-B6 (FC, register loss)".

2. Line 859: replace "The BQ25773 is the only part whose FC bound after a register loss exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop and the 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX." with "After a register loss the BQ25773 FC bound (1.39 A) exceeds the 1.20 A arm drop and the 1.247 A H-20 P_MAX. The BQ25798 FC bound after an IINDPM loss stays at 1.15 A only if ILIM_HIZ is re-sampled after qualification (P6, S1-B6); in the branch the datasheet describes (an ADC read at POR and at each qualification, held as an IINDPM clamp, SLUSDV2C section 7.3.4 step 6 and section 7.3.4.3 p.27) the bound is 1.70-1.76 A."

3. Section 0.1 reason 2 (lines 69-71): delete the clause "and its fuel-cell (FC) bus draw after a loss of its input-current register reaches 1.39 A, above the 1.20 A arm drop ([D773] sections 8.3, 11.4)" and cite only [D773] section 8.3; or, if it is kept, append "(the BQ25798 shares this hazard class at 1.70-1.76 A unless bench gate S1-B6 shows continuous ILIM_HIZ re-sampling)". The lead sentence "Each challenger brings a hazard that the BQ25798 does not have" then rests on tTRANS_CHG alone for the BQ25773, which is unaffected.

4. Section 5.1 HR codes (line 578): after "FC bound after a POR 1.15 A, margin 0.05 A to the 1.20 A arm drop" insert "; after an IINDPM register loss the FC bound is conditional on P6 / S1-B6: 1.15 A if re-sampled, 1.70-1.76 A if sampled once".

Mirror: scoring.md:174-175 (observation three under Table 5) takes the same replacement as edit 2.

Proof of the corrected numbers: 1.55 + 0.15 = 1.70 A and 1.61 + 0.15 = 1.76 A from the dossier's own baseline band (dossier_BQ25798.md:276-277) and aux term (:264); 1.76 - 1.20 = 0.56 A; 1.76 - 1.40 = 0.36 A; 1.003 + 0.15 = 1.153 A reproduces the retained POR figure. The branch wording quotes SLUSDV2C p.27 verbatim. No score or rank changes (score.py has no HR4 term; S3 already 2 for the BQ25798), so no re-run of score.py is required. Fix class: documentation correction with an existing bench gate (S1-B6, charger_replacement_evaluation_2026-11-05.md:911 already records "whether the pin is sampled once or continuously"); no firmware test, pinmap, protocol, or coefficient follow-on.
- Adjacent findings: P6 is framed in the dossier and [EVAL] as a datasheet 'contradiction' between the pin table and section 7.3.4.3; on reading SLUSDV2C p.5 and p.27 the two passages are consistent with a single mechanism (ADC read at POR/qualification held as an IINDPM clamp; 'the host cannot program the IINDPM register to any values higher than this ILIM clamp after POR'). No datasheet text describes continuous re-sampling, so the document's default branch should be the sampled-once one until S1-B6 shows otherwise; the current prose treats the favourable branch as the default.; REG06 IINDPM is reset by REG_RST only (SLUSDV2C Table 7-18 p.63), not by the watchdog; the BQ25798 'register loss' paths for the FC bound are therefore REG_RST, a stray REG06 write, or EN_EXTILIM = 0 (REG14[1]). Neither the target nor dossier_BQ25798.md names the paths, so the comparison with the BQ25773 'IIN_HOST lost (POR value 5 A)' row is not shown to be like-for-like (whether IIN_HOST is watchdog-reset on the BQ25773 was not checked here).; scoring.md:174-175 carries the identical unconditional 'only part' sentence; the working document needs the same correction as the target.; Nit: Table 6.0b and section 5.1 print the FC margin as 0.05 A where dossier_BQ25798.md:266 gives 0.047 A; the rounding hides that the margin is below any plausible pin-conversion error, which the dossier states explicitly.
- Counter-review: REFINE (major): Major confirmed; I accept the correction to my own figure. The like-for-like bound includes the 0.15 A aux the target's 1.15 A and the BQ25773's 1.39 A both carry: 1.55-1.61 A + 0.15 A = 1.70-1.76 A, above the 1.20 A arm drop by 0.50-0.56 A, above the 1.4 A OC_FC latch by 0.30-0.36 A, and 0.31-0.37 A above the figure held against the BQ25773 alone. Reachability holds: the designed Run-entry qualification is in the regen divider position and the Regen -> FC transition never re-qualifies, and SLUSDV2C 7.3.4.3 describes only the held-clamp behaviour, so the sampled-once branch is at least as likely as the one the target assumes. Not critical because the OC_FC raw-sample latch fires before any converter path and S3 already discounts P6 so no score moves. The four edits plus the scoring.md mirror are accepted; the register-loss path note (REG_RST, stray REG06 write, EN_EXTILIM = 0; watchdog does not reset REG06) belongs in the Table 6.0b cell.

### CIS-R1-07 (facts; reviewer major; final major; accepted)

- Location: docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:536 (Table 5.1 BQ25773 row); also :78-80 (section 0.1 reason 4), :550-551 (section 5.0 bullet), :620-621 (section 5.2), :848 (Table 6.1 row)
- Claim: BQ25773 delivers 3.04 A at an 8.4 V pack at the threshold floor (ICHG low corner of the 3.200 A setting); 'The shortfall is a setting choice. The 3.264 A setting closes it'; reason 4: 'A 3.264 A setting closes the BQ25773 gap; the BQ25690 gap cannot close without a regen ceiling above the HR4 hard-stop row.'
- Problem: The target defines the delivered current with every tolerance at its low corner and the regen VINDPM at its floor (lines 125-127). At 8.4 V the BQ25773 is limited by its input ceiling, not by ICHG. [D773] 7.3 gives a regen ceiling band of 1.30-1.79 A; [D773] 11.3 puts the 8.4 V low-corner input at 24.8 W (1.32 A x 18.81 V), or 24.5 W with the leakage term (1.30 A). Delivered = 1.30 x 18.81 x 0.95 / 8.4 = 2.77 A (2.80 A at 1.32 A; 2.78 A at eta 0.955): 0.33 A short, not 0.06 A. Raising ICHG to 3.264 A changes nothing because the ceiling binds. A guaranteed 3.1 A needs a ceiling minimum of 3.1 x 8.4 / (0.95 x 18.81) = 1.457 A; with the +-0.244 A pin, divider and leakage band the maximum becomes about 1.95 A, and the constant-margin allowance falls to 0.757 V x 0.4 mF / 1.95 A - 59 us = about 96 us, below the 110-115 us pass value. The BQ25773 shortfall is therefore structural in exactly the BQ25690 sense. Exposure: silently wrong published numbers, and the rule of succession (sections 7 and 6.4 item 5 single out the BQ25690 for the shortfall 'which the operator must accept explicitly') does not hold.
- Reviewer recommendation: Recompute the BQ25773 row as min(ICHG low corner, ceiling minimum x VINDPM floor x eta / 8.4) = about 2.77 A, and mark it 'Not met (-0.33 A), structural'. Delete 'the 3.264 A setting closes it'. Restate reason 4 and section 5.0 so that both TI challengers carry a structural ceiling-bound shortfall. Re-examine the 'Burst / shortfall' differentiators in the rule of succession.
- Verdict: CONFIRMED, severity major
- Evidence: The target defines "delivered at the threshold floor" as the worst-case current at an 8.4 V pack with every tolerance at its low corner and the regen VINDPM at its floor (charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:125-127). For the BQ25798 row and the BQ25690 row, the delivered figure is taken as min(ICHG limit, ceiling limit): dossier_BQ25798.md:393-401 gives 2.91 A, ceiling-limited, at 8.4 V; dossier_BQ25690.md:587 gives 2.62 A, input-limited. The BQ25773 row alone uses only the ICHG low corner, 3.04 A (target :536, :78-80, :620-621, :848; scoring.md:163).

The BQ25773 dossier's own numbers contradict that row:
- dossier_BQ25773:301-312 (section 7.3) gives a regen ceiling band of 1.30-1.79 A, or 1.32 A without the leakage term.
- dossier_BQ25773:526-536 (section 11.3), row "Pack 8.4 V, low corners", gives a ceiling limit of 24.8 W and a charge limit of 26.9 W. The ceiling therefore binds at 8.4 V.
- The sentence at dossier :536, "With the leakage term ... 24.5 W, so the charge limit still governs", holds only for the 7.4 V row. At 8.4 V, 24.5 W is less than 26.9 W, so the ceiling governs.

Raising the CHARGE_CURRENT setting to 3.264 A (dossier section 11.1, :500-509) raises only the non-binding term. The claim "The 3.264 A setting closes it" (target :550-551, :536, :79-80, :621) is therefore false.

To close the gap with the ceiling instead, the ceiling maximum must rise above both the 1.72 A HR4 hard-stop row (dossier_BQ25773:370; target :848) and the 110 us bench pass value (target :612). Target :546-549 calls this condition "structural" for the BQ25690.
- Quantification: Delivered current at an 8.4 V pack, with VINDPM low edge 18.81 V and eta 0.95 (dossier section 11.3 basis):
- Ceiling 1.32 A (no leakage): 1.32 x 18.81 = 24.83 W, x 0.95 / 8.4 = 2.808 A.
- Ceiling 1.30 A (with leakage): 24.45 W, giving 2.766 A.
- At eta 0.955: 2.78-2.82 A.
- Delivered = min(3.04 A ICHG, 2.77-2.81 A ceiling) = 2.77-2.81 A. The shortfall is 0.29-0.33 A, not the published 0.06 A.

A 3.264 A setting gives min(3.10, 2.77) = 2.77 A, so it changes nothing.

Required ceiling minimum for 3.1 A: 3.1 x 8.4 / (0.95 x 18.81) = 1.457 A. The nominal setting is then 1.457 + 0.200 (pin) + 0.029 (divider, 0.7 % x 1.68 V / 0.4) + 0.016 (leakage) = 1.702 A. The maximum is 1.702 + 0.245 = 1.947 A.

Hard-stop allowance on the dossier's own I x t basis, using the EVAL rows at 201-230 A.us:
- At 1.947 A: 201 / 1.947 = 103 us, up to 118 us at the 230 A.us end. The low end is below the 110 us bench pass.
- Cross-check at 1.79 A: 201 / 1.79 = 112.3 us, which matches the dossier's "about 112 us".
- The finding's 96 us comes from a different formula (0.757 V x 0.4 mF / I - 59 us) that is not traceable to the working documents. The direction holds and the magnitude differs.

The 1.947 A maximum is also 13 % above the 1.72 A hard-stop row.
- Source: Working documents only; no primary datasheet fetch was needed, because the error is internal arithmetic consistency:
- dossier_BQ25773_SMBus_twin_BQ25770G.md, section 7.3 (ceiling band, with the DS EC p.12 pin accuracy of ±200 mA) and section 11.3 (the input-power table).
- dossier_BQ25798.md, section 11.3, and dossier_BQ25690.md:587, which show the min(ICHG, ceiling) convention the target applies to the other rows.
- The target's own definition at :125-127.
- Severity rationale: This is a silently wrong published number. The decision table states a 0.06 A shortfall, and the recomputed shortfall is 0.29-0.33 A, about 5x larger. The table also states a remedy (the 3.264 A setting) that is computably ineffective, because the ceiling term binds: min(3.10, 2.77) = 2.77 A.

The error also flips a qualitative differentiator the operator is asked to act on. Section 5.0 and the rule of succession (:1067-1068) single out the BQ25690 for a shortfall "which the operator must accept explicitly". In fact the BQ25773, which leads while T-5.HIZ is open, carries the same structural class: closing its gap pushes the ceiling maximum to about 1.95 A, which is 103 us of hard-stop allowance against the 110 us bench pass.

This is not critical. No kill path or back-feed is created, and the ceiling still limits the input. The weighted scores are unchanged, because no criterion scores delivered current (target :553-555). The BQ25798 recommendation stands, because the BQ25798 delivers 3.10 A with the reference-biased ceiling.
- Minimal fix: 1. **Table 5.1, BQ25773 row (:536).**
   - Column 3 becomes: "2.77 A: input-limited at the regen ceiling minimum (1.30 A x 18.81 V, eta 0.95); the ICHG low corner (3.04 A at the 3.200 A setting) does not bind".
   - Column 4 becomes: "**Not met** (-0.33 A); a higher CHARGE_CURRENT setting does not close it".

2. **Section 5.0 bullet (:550-551).** Replace it with: "**BQ25773.** The shortfall is structural. With the ±0.245 A pin, divider and leakage band of [D773] section 7.3, the maximum becomes about 1.95 A, above the 1.72 A hard-stop row, with an allowance of about 103 us against the 110 us bench pass (C)."

3. **Following paragraph (:555).** Change "a disadvantage of the BQ25690" to "a disadvantage of the BQ25690 and the BQ25773".

5. **Section 5.2 (:620-621).** Replace with: "Charge at an 8.4 V pack: 2.77 A, input-limited at the regen ceiling minimum, below 3.1 A; a higher charge-current setting does not close it (Table 5.1)."

6. **Table 6.1 (:848).** Change to "2.77 A at the ceiling minimum; below 3.1 A".

7. **Section 7 (:1067-1068).** Add: "The BQ25773 lead carries a 2.77 A shortfall of the same structural class, which the operator must also accept explicitly."

8. **Upstream (working document).** In dossier_BQ25773 section 11.3, line 536, append "at a 7.4 V pack; at 8.4 V the ceiling governs (24.5 W, 2.77 A delivered)". Mirror the change in scoring.md:163.

Proof: each figure is recomputed in the QUANTIFICATION field from the dossier's own inputs (sections 7.3 and 11.3). No follow-on firmware skill is triggered, because this is a doc-only fix. The fix is a re-derivation of a document number.
- Adjacent findings: dossier_BQ25773 section 11.3 line 536 states 'the charge limit still governs' with the leakage term, but its own 8.4 V row shows the ceiling (24.8 W) is below the charge limit (26.9 W). This is the root cause of the Table 5.1 error.; The finding's hard-stop formula '0.757 V x 0.4 mF / I - 59 us' does not appear in the dossiers. The dossier's I x t basis (201-230 A.us) gives 103-118 us at 1.95 A. The conclusion holds and the reviewer's 96 us figure is not traceable.; The BQ25773 regen ceiling maximum is already 1.79 A, above the 1.72 A hard-stop row. Target :848 uses '1.72 A row' as the BQ25690's structural bar, but the BQ25773 already sits outside it and relies on the 110 us bench pass. The two parts are not held to the same bar in section 5.0.
- Counter-review: REFINE (major): Major confirmed. I defend my 96 us allowance figure against the verifier's 103 us. The verifier derived 103 us from the I x t product of the 1.72 A EVAL row (201 A us / 1.947 A); the R1-33 verdict in this same round shows that the EVAL 2.5 rows are constant-charge, not constant I x t (Q = (t + 59 us) x I = 302.7-303.8 uC for all three rows, reproducing the [D798] 129 us and 108 us baseline values), and that carrying the 1.72 A product to a higher current is non-conservative. On the EVAL-consistent model the allowance at the 1.947 A ceiling maximum is 302.8 uC / 1.947 A - 59 us = 96.5 us, so my figure is the traceable one; the verifier's 103 us is an overstatement of about 7 us in the non-conservative direction. Both values sit below the 110 us bench pass, so the conclusion is unchanged either way: a guaranteed 3.1 A at 8.4 V needs a ceiling minimum of 1.457 A, the +-0.245 A band gives a 1.947 A maximum (13 % above the 1.72 A row), and the BQ25773 shortfall is structural in the BQ25690 sense. The published 0.06 A shortfall is 5x low (true 0.29-0.33 A at min(3.04, 2.77-2.81)), and the published remedy (3.264 A setting) is computably ineffective because the ceiling binds. Fix item 2 should quote 96 us (or '96-103 us depending on model') rather than 103 us, and the Table 5.1 'Against' column must be re-based per S01.

### CIS-R1-09 (decision; reviewer major; final minor; accepted)

- Location: charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:225 (condition 1), :831-834 (Table 6.0a), :928 (Table 6.4); scoring.md:148-151
- Claim: BQ25690: "Open: M5 is B where the BQ25798 has P, until bench gate T-5.HIZ passes"; LTC4015 and RAA489110: "Fails: M5 conditional where the BQ25798 has P". BQ25773 M7: "Met under a symmetric M7 grading: the BQ25798 M7 is itself conditional".
- Problem: The BQ25798 does not have P evidence for M5 in its own dossier. dossier_BQ25798.md section 3 grades M5 "Pass (P; EN_EXTILIM interaction B)" and states that whether EN_EXTILIM = 0 "leaves the < 0.75 V stop is an inference ... (B: S1-B3)"; verification-log item 12 records "M5 marked B; check added to S1-B3". The only BQ25798 pin that stops conversion is ILIM_HIZ (/CE "disables charging" while the converter keeps regulating SYS, the behaviour for which the BQ25690 review finding 18 withdrew that part's M5 P). For the sibling BQ25690 the datasheet states that EN_EXTILIM = 0 also disables HIZ through the pin (dossier_BQ25690.md line 920, SLUSFN4A 6.3.8.1.2.1 p.26), so the BQ25798 inference is the pessimistic reading. The BQ25798 conversion-stopping pin is therefore in the same register-defeatable class as the RAA489110 BATGONE (Control4 bit 15, POR 0, readback) and the BQ25798 EN_EXTILIM (POR 1, readback); the M5 pass condition explicitly admits that class. The dossier_RAA...md line 724 basis ("BQ25798 has P evidence for M5 through a /CE pin that no register defeats") is wrong on the first clause. Under the symmetric grading applied to M7 for the BQ25773: RAA489110 condition 1 is met (it still fails condition 3), BQ25690 condition 1 is met or equal-status (B vs B), and the LTC4015 fails on M3 F alone. Exposure: Table 0.2 ranks 3 and 5 statuses, the BQ25690 "pending T-5.HIZ" qualifier, the rule of succession, and the T-5.HIZ gate's claimed power to "close or fail condition 1" all change.
- Reviewer recommendation: Grade condition 1 with the BQ25798 status exactly as its dossier records it (M5 B, M7 c). Restate Table 6.0a with a BQ25798 column of must-have statuses and apply one reading to every row. Either confirm the EN_EXTILIM/HIZ interaction from SLUSDV2C section 7.3.4.3 (or TI E2E) and upgrade the BQ25798 M5 to P with the citation, or re-grade the BQ25690 and RAA489110 condition 1 and propagate to Tables 0.2, 6.4, section 7 and 8.2.
- Verdict: PARTLY_CONFIRMED, severity minor
- Evidence: **Confirmed parts.**
(1) The BQ25798 dossier does not record an unqualified P for M5. `docs/reviews/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10/dossier_BQ25798.md:122` grades M5 "**Pass (P; EN_EXTILIM interaction B)**" and states that EN_EXTILIM = 0 leaving the < 0.75 V stop "is an inference ... (B: S1-B3)"; `dossier_BQ25798.md:641` (verification log 12) records "M5 marked B; check added to S1-B3"; `dossier_BQ25798.md:549` carries the S1-B3 bench item. The target's Table 6.0a (`charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:830`) and `scoring.md:147` list the BQ25798 conditional passes as M2, M4, M7, M9, M10 and omit M5, and rows 831/833/834 and Table 6.4 (`:928`) use the clause "where the BQ25798 has P" without the qualifier.
(2) The primary datasheet leaves the interaction open, exactly as the dossier says. SLUSDV2C (fetched 2026-11-10 from ti.com, revised June 2026) p.27 §7.3.4.3 gives the clamp paragraph ("the host cannot program the IINDPM register to any values higher than this ILIM clamp after POR, unless the register bit EN_EXTILIM is set to 0") and the stop paragraph ("when the ILIM_HIZ pin is pulled lower than 0.75V, the charger stops switching and REGN stays on. The charger resumes switching if the ILIM_HIZ pin voltage becomes higher than 1V") as separate paragraphs with no stated interaction. p.78 Table 7-30 describes EN_EXTILIM only as "Enable the external ILIM_HIZ pin input current regulation", POR 1b, reset by REG_RST only. By contrast the sibling SLUSFN4A p.26 §6.3.8.1.2.1 states explicitly: "Disable the ILIM_HIZ pin function by setting the EN_EXTILIM bit to 0. When the pin is disabled, input current limit and monitoring functions, as well as HIZ mode control through the pin, are not available." So the pessimistic reading for the BQ25798 is plausible by sibling analogy and unresolved by SLUSDV2C; the finding's proposal to "confirm from SLUSDV2C section 7.3.4.3" cannot close it, only TI E2E or bench S1-B3 can.
(3) The conversion-stopping pin on the BQ25798 is ILIM_HIZ, not /CE. SLUSDV2C p.5 Table 5-1: /CE "Battery charging is enabled when EN_CHG bit is 1 and CE pin is LOW"; ILIM_HIZ "When the pin voltage is below 0.75V, the buck-boost converter enters non-switching mode, similar to HiZ mode using EN_HIZ bit, but with REGN on." Therefore the RAA dossier's basis sentence (`dossier_RAA489110...md:109` and `:724`: "The BQ25798 has P evidence for M5 through a /CE pin that no register defeats") rests on the wrong pin; the same reasoning withdrew the BQ25690's /CE-based M5 P (`dossier_BQ25690.md:920`, log 18). The RAA489110 M5 conditional is the register-defeat class (Control4 bits 15/14, POR 0, readback, `dossier_RAA...md:109`), which [CRIT] M5 expressly admits (`screening_criteria.md:69`: "A register bit that can disable the pin's function is acceptable only if the POR value enables the pin and the never-write list plus readback can hold it"), and on which the BQ25798's own status is B. So "M5 conditional where the BQ25798 has P" is not established for the RAA489110 on that clause.

**Refuted part.** The claim that the BQ25690 condition 1 is "met or equal-status (B vs B)" conflates two different sub-questions. The BQ25690 M5 B (`dossier_BQ25690.md:107,117-119`) concerns the published HIZ entry level itself (VIH_ILIM_HIZ has a MIN of 1.77 V only), whose worst case is that the CCB drive does not enter HIZ at all (no conversion-stopping pin in the design). The BQ25798's B concerns the EN_EXTILIM register interaction, whose worst case is a register-defeatable pin that [CRIT] M5 still passes with POR 1 + never-write + readback. On the sub-claim the BQ25690 is conditional on (a published stop level), the BQ25798 has P (0.75 V / 1 V, SLUSDV2C p.27, p.5). `dossier_BQ25690.md:118` already states the comparison this narrowly. The T-5.HIZ gate and the "Open" status therefore stand; only the target's wording is broader than the dossier basis.

**Outcome impact.** Scores and condition 2 are unaffected: margins +8.0 / +8.7 / +8.3 / -4.7 (`charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:929`) all fail the 10-point test, so no part can be recommended over the BQ25798 on any reading of condition 1. The RAA489110 still fails condition 3 (`:930`) and sits -4.7 below the baseline; the LTC4015 still fails M3 F (`:833`); the BQ25773 is unaffected. The rule of succession (`:1058-1065`) ranks the BQ25773 and BQ25690 by T-5.HIZ, which survives, and never reaches the RAA489110.
- Quantification: Not a numeric finding; the load-bearing values recomputed/re-read from the primary source are: BQ25798 ILIM_HIZ stop threshold 0.75 V, resume 1 V (SLUSDV2C p.27, p.5) - a published level, P; EN_EXTILIM POR 1b, reset by REG_RST only, description limited to "input current regulation" (p.78 Table 7-30) - interaction with the stop unstated, B; BQ25690 EN_EXTILIM = 0 removes "HIZ mode control through the pin" (SLUSFN4A p.26) - explicit. Decision-rule arithmetic unchanged: margins 48.3 - 40.3 = +8.0, 49.0 - 40.3 = +8.7, 48.7 - 40.3 = +8.3, 35.7 - 40.3 = -4.7; all < 10, so condition 2 fails for every challenger independent of condition 1. The RAA489110 would need +14.7 points to reach the margin; no re-grading of M5 moves a score (condition 1 is a gate, not a scored item).
- Source: Primary datasheets: SLUSDV2C (BQ25798, rev June 2026) p.5 Table 5-1, p.27 §7.3.4.3, p.78 Table 7-30; SLUSFN4A (BQ25690, rev Dec 2025) p.21 §6.3.3, p.26 §6.3.8.1.2.1 - both fetched from ti.com into the scratch directory C:\Users\ricky\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\53f5790609a5edc5\e7982b37-24d6-4881-9fb4-c0e5262cc0ba\scratchpad\advrev\dl\. Working documents: screening_criteria.md:69 (M5 text), :158 (condition 1); dossier_BQ25798.md:122, :549, :641; dossier_BQ25690.md:107, :117-119, :920; dossier_RAA489110...md:109, :724, :787; dossier_BQ25773...md:769, :828; scoring.md:145-151. No calc script encodes condition 1 (grep of calc/ for "condition|M5|must" returned nothing), so the clause lives in prose only.
- Severity rationale: Doc drift / overstated basis: the target states a decision-rule premise ("the BQ25798 has P") more strongly than its own dossier records (P on the stop level, B on the register interaction) and one dossier sentence names the wrong pin. No exposure path: no hardware kill path, no circular loop, no wrong published number, and no decision the operator would make differently - every challenger fails condition 2 by 1.3-14.7 points, the RAA489110 fails condition 3 and the LTC4015 fails M3 F regardless, and the BQ25690 "pending T-5.HIZ" status survives on the narrowed (correct) reading because its B is on a different, more severe sub-claim (unpublished stop level, worst case no pin inhibit) than the BQ25798's B (register interaction, worst case a CRIT-admitted conditional). The ranking, the alternate, the rule of succession and the recommendation are unchanged. The one real consequence - S1-B3 can downgrade the BQ25798's M5 to the register-defeat class and thereby remove the M5 discriminator against the RAA489110 - still leaves the RAA489110 last on score and condition 3.
- Minimal fix: Four text edits, no number changes, no test/pinmap/protocol/coeff follow-on (doc-only class):
1. `charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:830` and `scoring.md:147`, BQ25798 "Conditional passes" cell: append "M5 (stop level 0.75 V / 1 V P, SLUSDV2C p.27; the EN_EXTILIM = 0 interaction with the stop is B, bench S1-B3; EN_EXTILIM POR 1, reset by REG_RST only, Table 7-30 p.78)".
2. `:831` / `scoring.md:148`, BQ25690 condition-1 cell: replace "M5 is B where the BQ25798 has P" with "M5 is B on the published HIZ entry level (VIH_ILIM_HIZ MIN only), where the BQ25798 has P (0.75 V / 1 V); the BQ25798's own M5 B (EN_EXTILIM interaction, S1-B3) is a different sub-claim that [CRIT] M5 admits with readback". Same substitution in Table 6.4 `:928` column 1: "Open (M5 B on the entry level, T-5.HIZ)".
3. `:834` / `scoring.md:151`, RAA489110 condition-1 cell: replace "**Fails**: M5 conditional where the BQ25798 has P" with "**Not decided on M5**: the BATGONE register defeat (Control4 bits 15, 14; POR 0; readback) is the class [CRIT] M5 admits and on which the BQ25798 is itself B (S1-B3); condition 3 fails (finding 8) and the score is -4.7". Table 6.4 `:928` RAA cell: "Not decided (M5 c, same class as the BQ25798 S1-B3 item)"; LTC4015 cell: "Fails (M3 F)" - the M5 clause is surplus. `:833` LTC4015: drop ", and M5 conditional where the BQ25798 has P".
4. `dossier_RAA489110...md:109` and `:724`: replace "through a /CE pin that no register defeats" with "through ILIM_HIZ, whose 0.75 V stop level is published (SLUSDV2C p.27); its EN_EXTILIM interaction is B ([B798] section 3, S1-B3). The EN_EXTILIM analogy holds for M5 as well as M7". Optionally reconcile `dossier_BQ25798.md:641` ("M5 marked B") with row :122 ("Pass (P; ... B)") to one wording: "M5 kept P on the stop level; the EN_EXTILIM interaction marked B".
5. Section 8.2 test table (`:1096` region): add to the S1-B3 row "If EN_EXTILIM = 0 removes the < 0.75 V stop, the BQ25798 M5 becomes a conditional pass of the register-defeat class; the RAA489110 M5 clause of condition 1 is then met (its outcome is unchanged by condition 3 and the -4.7 margin)".
Proof: each replacement cites the datasheet page re-read above; the outcomes columns (Alternate / Alternate pending T-5.HIZ / Not eligible / Below the baseline) are untouched because conditions 2 and 3 and M3 F decide them independently of M5.
- Adjacent findings: Inconsistent application of [CRIT] M5 'stops conversion' across dossiers: the RAA489110 BATGONE acts on Q5 only and the converter keeps regulating VSYS_L (dossier_RAA489110...md:127, :160, :178), the same property for which the BQ25690's /CE was withdrawn as the M5 pin (dossier_BQ25690.md:920), yet the RAA M5 is graded 'P mechanism' (:109). Applied uniformly, the RAA489110 M5 would be conditional on a sub-claim where the BQ25798 does have P (ILIM_HIZ stops switching), restoring the target's M5 discriminator on a different basis; outcome still unchanged.; SLUSDV2C Table 7-30 p.78: EN_EXTILIM is reset by REG_RST only, not by WATCHDOG (unlike the BQ25690, whose dossier :107/:109 says the watchdog restores it). A corrupted EN_EXTILIM = 0 on the BQ25798 therefore persists through a watchdog expiry; only readback, REG_RST or POR restores it. The BQ25798 dossier M7/HR4 rows state 'POR 1' and readback but do not note the watchdog does not restore it.; dossier_BQ25798.md internal inconsistency: row :122 headline 'Pass (P; EN_EXTILIM interaction B)' versus verification log :641 'M5 marked B' - the two readings are the root of this finding and should be reconciled to one wording.; The target cites dossier_BQ25690.md:118 for the BQ25690 M5 comparison but drops that line's qualifier ('its ILIM_HIZ < 0.75 V stops switching is a published level'), which is the narrow and correct form of the clause.
- Counter-review: CONCEDE (minor): The verifier's re-read of the primary separates the two sub-claims correctly: the BQ25798 stop level 0.75 V / 1 V is published (P), the EN_EXTILIM interaction with the stop is unstated (B, S1-B3), and the BQ25690's B is on a more severe sub-claim (unpublished entry level). Condition 2 fails for every challenger by 1.3-14.7 points independent of condition 1, the RAA489110 fails condition 3 and the LTC4015 fails M3 F, so no outcome column changes. The dossier_RAA sentence naming a /CE pin 'that no register defeats' is wrong on the pin and should be corrected. Minor; all five edits accepted.

### CIS-R1-10 (coverage; reviewer major; final minor; accepted)

- Location: Section 3.4 'No datasheet' bullet (l.430-431); [SCR] section 3 row 'TI part numbers without a datasheet'
- Claim: 'TI part numbers that return HTTP 404 (BQ25770 non-G, BQ25680, BQ25856, ...; assumed non-existent, UNVERIFIED)'
- Problem: The BQ25856-Q1 exists. TI SLUSFH5A (Oct 2024) describes it as a 70 V-operating, 85 V absolute-maximum, I2C-controlled bidirectional buck-boost charge controller, pin-compatible with the BQ25756. The 404 came from omitting the '-q1' suffix in the symlink URL. The part needs at least the BQ25756 M9 screening (likely the same core), not a non-existence dismissal. The URL-form defect may also apply to the other 404 entries. This dismissal rests on a fetch artefact, not on a primary.
- Reviewer recommendation: Re-fetch the 404 part numbers with their orderable suffixes (bq25856-q1.pdf). Screen the BQ25856-Q1 at least alongside the BQ25756 (M9). Correct section 3.4 so that 404 entries are 'not located', not 'assumed non-existent'.
- Verdict: PARTLY_CONFIRMED, severity minor
- Evidence: PREMISE CONFIRMED (the part exists; the 404 is a URL-suffix artefact): curl probe of https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/bq25856-q1.pdf -> HTTP 200 (84-page PDF, saved at C:\Users\ricky\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\53f5790609a5edc5\e7982b37-24d6-4881-9fb4-c0e5262cc0ba\scratchpad\advrev\dl\bq25856-q1.pdf); bq25856.pdf -> 404. Header: "SLUSFH5A - MAY 2024 - REVISED OCTOBER 2024", "PRODUCTION DATA", title "BQ25856-Q1: Automotive, Standalone/I2C Controlled, 1- to 14-Cell Bidirectional Buck-Boost ... Charge Controller". Absolute maximum (p.7, section 6.1): VAC, ACUV, ACOV, ACP, ACN, SRP, SRN, FB, FBG -0.3 to 85 V. Recommended operating (p.7, section 6.3): VAC 4.4-70 V. Device Comparison (p.3, section 4): BQ25856-Q1 vs BQ25756 vs BQ25758A, all 36-pin 5x6 QFN, I2C address 0x6B, I2C + Standalone (same package and pin count; pin-for-pin compatibility not verified by me). ICHG_REG_RANGE 0.4-20 A (EC table), 16-bit ADC (p.1).

TARGET WORDING AT FAULT: C:/Life Ops/School/Thesis/DC-Balancer-charger-search/docs/charger_ic_search_2026-11-10.md:430-431 ("TI part numbers that return HTTP 404 (BQ25770 non-G, BQ25680, BQ25856, ... assumed non-existent, UNVERIFIED)"); [SCR] screening.md:117 (same row) and screening.md:229-230 ("bq25856.pdf: HTTP 404 (part number probably does not exist under that name)").

CONSEQUENCE REFUTED (the part does not survive the screen; it inherits the BQ25756 M9 F): SLUSFH5A section 7.3.3.3 p.22: "At startup, the device toggles the SW node for about 40 ms to determine the correct compensation values for a given set of passives." This is the sentence that rejects the BQ25756 (SLUSEN5A section 6.3.3.3 p.21, cited in [SCR] section 2.1 row 9, screening.md:70, and in the target Table 3.1 M9 row, l.397). SLUSFH5A p.65: "the minimum recommended capacitor for converter stability is 80 uF, so this minimum value should be used" (same C_IN floor as the BQ25756, [RES] rescreen_2026-11-05_candidates.md:307). SLUSFH5A Fig 8-7 p.66 ("Charge Enable via CE Pin with 5-A ICHG", rendered at 400 dpi): horizontal 40.0 ms/div; CE falls about 1.0 div after the trigger, ICHG rises about 3.3 div after it. The M9 reading rule is [SCR] section 1.2 item 2 (screening.md:31-34): a pin inhibit with a documented restart of 10 ms or longer fails M9. Sibling handling precedent: [SCR] row 8 (screening.md:69) rejects the BQ25822 on "M9 F expected (same core as the BQ25820)", so a same-core row for the BQ25856-Q1 is the established form.

OTHER 404 ENTRIES: probed with and without -q1: bq25855, bq25855-q1, bq25821(-q1), bq25823(-q1), bq25680(-q1), bq25770(-q1), bq25771/2/4(-q1), bq25792(-q1), bq25855a, bq25856a, bq25857-q1 -> all 404. bq25770g -> 200 (the BQ25770G of [D773], so the "BQ25770 non-G" entry is consistent). The URL-form defect is confirmed for exactly one of the nine listed part numbers.
- Quantification: M9 threshold (screening.md:31-34): restart >= 10 ms fails. BQ25856-Q1: start-up compensation toggle about 40 ms (SLUSFH5A p.22) = 4.0x the threshold on the text alone. Fig 8-7 p.66: (3.3 - 1.0) div x 40 ms/div = about 92 ms from the CE edge to charge current (figure reading; +/-0.5 div = +/-20 ms gives 72-112 ms), about 9x the threshold; the BQ25756 record reads 145 ms on its Fig 7-7 (dossier_A1.md:67), so the BQ25856-Q1 is of the same order, not warm. C_IN floor 80 uF (p.65) = the BQ25756 figure (rescreen:307). Ratings against the other must-haves: M2 85 V abs max / 70 V operating (pass); M4 0.4-20 A (pass); M10 I2C (pass). URL probes: 30 symlink forms tried, 7 return 200 (bq25856-q1, bq25858-q1, bq25756, bq25770g, bq25820, bq25822, bq25758a), 23 return 404; of the nine part numbers in the target's list, 1 of 9 exists under a suffixed form.
- Source: Primary datasheet TI SLUSFH5A (BQ25856-Q1, Oct 2024) p.3, p.7, p.22, p.65, p.66, fetched live from ti.com; cross-checked against [SCR] screening.md sections 1.2 (M9 reading), 2.1 rows 8-9, section 3 row 117 and the fetch log l.229; [RES] section 3.7 and l.306-308; round-2 dossier_A1.md l.64-68 for the BQ25756 comparison figures. No repo design file (IO CSV, BOM, schematic) bears on this finding.
- Severity rationale: The defect is a wrong stated basis for a dismissal (fetch artefact presented as non-existence), i.e. doc drift. Exposure path check: the re-admitted part fails the same must-have as its sibling by a 4-9x margin (40 ms toggle and about 92 ms CE-to-current against the 10 ms M9 rule), carries the same 80 uF input floor, and would at best join [SCR] row 9 as a rejected sibling; the shortlist, the scores and the BQ25798 recommendation are untouched. No part recommendation changes, no kill path, no circular loop, no published number is wrong, and the operator would not decide differently. Under the severity contract this caps at minor.
- Minimal fix: Target section 3.4, replace l.430-431 with:
"- **No datasheet located:** TI part numbers whose ti.com/lit/ds/symlink form returns HTTP 404 with and without the -q1 suffix (BQ25770 non-G, BQ25680, BQ25821, BQ25823, BQ25855, BQ25771/2/4; not located, UNVERIFIED). The BQ25856-Q1 (SLUSFH5A, October 2024) was located under its orderable suffix. It is a BQ25756-family buck-boost controller (85 V absolute maximum, 4.4-70 V operating, I2C, 0.4-20 A) with the same 40 ms start-up compensation toggle (SLUSFH5A section 7.3.3.3 p.22), the same 80 uF minimum input capacitance (p.65) and about 90 ms from the /CE edge to charge current (Fig 8-7 p.66, 40 ms/div). It fails M9 with the BQ25756 ([SCR] section 2.1 row 9)."

[SCR] screening.md:229-230: change to "bq25792.pdf, bq25770.pdf, bq25680.pdf: HTTP 404 with and without -q1 (not located). bq25856.pdf: HTTP 404; bq25856-q1.pdf: HTTP 200 (screened, row 9a)."
Proof: every number in the replacement text is read from SLUSFH5A at the cited page (85 V p.7; 4.4-70 V p.7; 0.4-20 A EC table; 40 ms p.22; 80 uF p.65; 40 ms/div and about 2.3 div p.66), and the M9 verdict follows from [SCR] section 1.2 item 2 applied to 40 ms >= 10 ms. Fix class: documentation only; no follow-on skill (no test, pinmap, protocol, coefficient or bench action) is triggered.
- Adjacent findings: BQ25858-Q1 / BQ25858B-Q1 (TI SLUSFH4A, March 2025 rev July 2025, PRODUCTION DATA; https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/bq25858-q1.pdf -> HTTP 200) is absent from the sweep: 4.4-70 V operating, I2C, 16-bit ADC, bidirectional buck-boost controller with output CC-CV, 400 mA-20 A, 36-pin 5x6 QFN. Its compensation section 7.3.3.4 does NOT carry the 40 ms start-up toggle sentence (the 80 uF stability floor remains, p.55), but it is a DC/DC controller with a 5 V default output and TS disabled, the class the sweep dismissed for the BQ25758A ('DC/DC without charge profile', [SCR] section 3). It needs at least a Table 3.2-style entry (scope dismissal or M9 screen); its start-up timing figures were not read (UNVERIFIED).; [SCR] screening.md:229-230 attributes the four 404s to non-existent part numbers; for bq25856 the cause is the missing -q1 suffix, so the fetch log is wrong for that entry (covered by the minimal fix).; The BQ25856-Q1 register map keeps EN_CHG POR default 1 (SLUSFH5A register table, EN_CHG 'R/W 0x1'), the same autonomous-charging-when-/CE-low default the BQ25756 carries (M6 c); relevant only if the part is ever re-screened past M9.
